## M4-DL-002 — M4-P1 operability (folded in pre-commit): parameters-as-data config surface + signal taxonomy/emission seam

- **Date.** 2026-06-24
- **Decision.** Fold two **data-discipline** operability surfaces into M4-P1 before commit (an extension on top of the standing, green base; not a redo). Owner-amended scope; UI/service explicitly out.
  1. **Parameters-as-data (config surface).** Add a typed surface `theygrow_api.parameters` layered over the M2 infra `Settings` (`config.py` stays infra-only). Each knob is a `Parameter` descriptor (`name`, `value`, `type_label`, `scope`, `changed_in`, `note`); `current_parameters()` is the render-ready data shape (a pure accessor, **not** a view/endpoint — the later read-only "current parameters + when they changed" view RENDERS it). P1 migrates the knobs it introduces into the surface: **`fts_config`** (`'simple'`, with its M4-DL-001 limitation/port-out note) and **`sparse_candidate_limit`** (the sparse leg's default cap, env-overridable). The sparse leg now defaults its `limit` from the surface rather than a scattered literal.
  2. **Two orthogonal provenance axes (correction).** `PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` is the **surface-STRUCTURE** version — it bumps when the registry's shape / the `Parameter` descriptor changes, **never** on a value change. Per-parameter **`changed_in`** is **value** provenance — the decision-log id that last set a knob's value (`fts_config` → `M4-DL-001`; `sparse_candidate_limit` default → `M4-DL-002`). The two are explicitly **not** interchangeable; the later render must not conflate "surface structure changed" with "this value changed".
  3. **`fts_config` is schema-bound; migration immutability (correction).** The generated `chunk_text_tsv` tsvector config is **frozen** as the literal `'simple'` in migration `0002`'s DDL **and** in `models.py`'s `Computed` — neither imports the mutable `parameters.FTS_CONFIG`. A migration is an immutable historical artifact: a later FTS port-out (e.g. `'russian'` at the recall trigger) is a **NEW migration** (a new revision whose DDL hardcodes the new literal) plus a matching surface value bump — **never** an edit to `0002` and never a lone `parameters.py` edit. The single link between the surface value and the real schema is the **drift guard** in `test_parameters` (`information_schema.generation_expression` carries `parameters.FTS_CONFIG`'s value); if the surface is bumped without a matching migration, that test fails.
  4. **Signal taxonomy + single emission seam.** Add `theygrow_api.signals`: a `SignalKind` taxonomy + `SIGNAL_TAXONOMY` registry defined NOW for `DERIVATION_COUNTERS`, `RETRIEVAL_LATENCY` (co-carries candidate count), `GROUNDING_COVERAGE`, `DEGRADATION_EVENT`; typed frozen `Signal`s with a `fields()` payload; one injectable `SignalSink` Protocol whose default `LoggingSignalSink` emits through the PII-guarded logging boundary (`logging.py`). P1 **emits** only what it produces: derivation counters (from `DeriveSummary`) and the sparse leg's candidate-count + latency. `GROUNDING_COVERAGE` / `DEGRADATION_EVENT` are **defined-not-emitted** — emitted when their producing code lands (P3/P4); no fabricated emitters.
  5. **§4 on signals.** Signal payloads carry **counts / ids / timings only** — never `raw_text` / `chunk_text`, and never family-identifying ids (`community_id`). Enforced by the typed `Signal.fields()` + `test_signals` (invariant `M4-P1-INV-003`).
  6. **Forward requirement.** P2 knobs (embedding model + final ≤1536 dimension) and P3 knobs (top_k / candidate_k, RRF weights) **MUST** land in this SAME config surface, and any new signals **MUST** emit through this SAME seam. Recorded now so the operability pattern is not re-litigated per packet.
- **Rationale.** Establishing the surface and seam at the first retrieval packet means the later read-only "current parameters + when they changed" view and operational signals are a render of structured data, not code archaeology or log-scraping — and P2/P3 inherit the pattern instead of retrofitting it. Keeping the migration's DDL a frozen literal preserves migration immutability (a config change is a schema event, surfaced by a new revision), while the drift guard keeps the mutable surface honest against the real schema. Separating surface-structure version from per-knob value provenance keeps "when did this value change" answerable per knob. The injectable sink makes emissions testable and leaves room for a metrics backend without touching producers. §4 holds: configuration and signals carry no child data.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Migration imports `parameters.FTS_CONFIG`.** Rejected (owner correction) — a later surface change would retroactively rewrite a frozen migration's meaning; the literal-in-DDL + drift-guard is the correct decoupling.
  2. **Single surface version as value provenance.** Rejected (owner correction) — conflates "surface structure changed" with "a value changed"; per-parameter `changed_in` is the value axis.
  3. **Structured-log-only emission (no sink abstraction).** Rejected — the injectable `SignalSink` is testable and swappable; its default still emits through the logging boundary, so nothing is lost.
  4. **Persist parameter history / signals in the DB.** Rejected — that is runtime state / write-admin, out of scope; the decision-log is the parameter-history trace and the default sink logs signals.
  5. **Wire the M3 importer's `RunSummary` through the seam now.** Rejected for P1 — it would modify already-committed M3 product code; P1 emits the derivation counters only. Wiring the importer through the same seam is a trivial future touch.
- **Supersedes.** None. Extends `M4-DL-001` (same packet; the `'simple'` limitation/port-out note now lives on the `fts_config` parameter). Builds on **ADR-007/M2-DL-001** (env-driven `Settings`), **ADR-005** (engine out of perimeter; `'simple'` faithful lift), **ADR-008** (one-Postgres store; the FTS recall limitation), and `M2-P2-INV-001` (the PII-guarded logging boundary the default sink reuses).
- **Effects.**
  - Adds `api/theygrow_api/parameters.py` (surface + `Parameter` + `RuntimeParameters` + `current_parameters()`) and `api/theygrow_api/signals.py` (taxonomy + `SignalSink` + `LoggingSignalSink`).
  - Relocates the mutable `FTS_CONFIG` to `parameters.py`; `db/models.py` and migration `0002` now hardcode the frozen literal `'simple'` (no import of the mutable constant); `retrieval/search_repository.py` reads `FTS_CONFIG` from the surface and defaults `limit` from `RuntimeParameters`; `derivation.py` and `search_repository.py` emit through the seam.
  - Adds `api/tests/test_parameters.py` (incl. the DB-backed drift guard + env-override) and `api/tests/test_signals.py`; extends `test_rederive.py` / `test_search_repository.py` with emission + default-limit assertions.
  - Enforced invariant `M4-P1-INV-003` (signal payloads are §4-safe).
  - **No schema change**: the `FTS_CONFIG` relocation is import-only; migration `0002`'s emitted DDL is byte-identical (`to_tsvector('simple', chunk_text)`).
  - **Findings (not forks):** the parser exposes **no numeric chunking knob** (line-based; one event line = one chunk — no window/overlap), so there is no chunking parameter to migrate; `DERIVE_ROUTES` ({note, draft}) is a **fork-b-pinned behavioral constant**, deliberately not a runtime knob (eligibility is the P3 retrieval-layer filter).
  - Out of this delta: P2/P3 knobs (recorded requirement only); `GROUNDING_COVERAGE` / `DEGRADATION_EVENT` emitters (P3/P4); the importer emitter; and any UI / read-only visibility surface / write-admin / auth / dashboards / metrics backend.

---

## M4-DL-003 — M4-P2 embeddings activation: provider-port lift, per-chunk vector + drift guard, fail-closed ZDR gate, index-after-backfill

- **Date.** 2026-06-24
- **Decision.** Activate the dense leg by **transferring** the engine embeddings adapter (ADR-005 §7: lift, not rewrite) under the approved owner fork (ADR-011, recorded in the vault), with these packet-level choices:
  1. **Provider behind the existing port.** Add an `EmbeddingProvider` Protocol + `EmbeddingBatch` result to `ports/provider.py`; lift the donor OpenAI adapter to `adapters/embeddings/openai_client.py` implementing it. The `openai` SDK is a **provider** dependency, NOT the `diary-memory-service` engine — there is no `memory_rag` import. The model (`text-embedding-3-large`, MRL-truncated) and dimension are config knobs behind the port, so it is swappable. The adapter DOES reach the cloud embedder; that egress of child `chunk_text` is admitted only under the owner-cleared ZDR + DPA + EU-residency surface (ADR-011 §1).
  2. **Per-chunk vector; dormant shell DROPPED (reconciliation, not a fork).** The dense vector lives on `event_chunks.embedding` (`vector(1536)`, the donor `embedding_records.chunk_id` analogue; ADR-011 §3 fixed per-CHUNK granularity). Migration `0003` adds it and **drops** the M3 reserved per-`source_message` `embedding` shell: it was unwritten and unindexed, so "migrate" was vacuous, and leaving it dormant would misread as "messages are embeddable". The dimension is the **FROZEN literal** `vector(1536)` in the `0003` DDL (and mirrored in `models.EMBEDDING_DIMENSION`); the mutable surface value is `parameters.EMBEDDING_DIMENSION`, and a new **drift guard** in `test_parameters` ties the surface to the live column type (`format_type` reads `vector(1536)`). Changing the dimension is a new-migration event (M4-DL-002 discipline) AND a re-embed.
  3. **Index AFTER backfill (owner correction).** Migration `0003` builds **no** HNSW index. The offline backfill creates `idx_event_chunks_embedding` (`hnsw`, `vector_cosine_ops`) AFTER bulk population, idempotently (`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`). Building HNSW on an all-NULL column then filling it row-by-row yields a worse graph; index-after-population is the default. **Trade-off recorded:** subsequent incremental runs add new rows to the existing graph (acceptable for a closed corpus's small tail); the bulk of the corpus is built in one shot.
  4. **Fail-closed ZDR gate = structural §4 enforcement (owner correction).** A new, default-less `embedder_privacy_cleared` infra flag (`Settings`; defaults FALSE = uncleared) gates the backfill: with it unset, the run refuses (`EmbedderNotReady`) **before** any provider call or DB write — NO `chunk_text` leaves the perimeter. The real path additionally requires embedder endpoint/key presence. This makes the ADR-011 §1 privacy surface a code gate, not prose.
  5. **Model-swap = re-embed (owner correction).** `embedding_model` is runtime/env-overridable, but "runtime" means **swappable-WITH-BACKFILL**: changing it against already-populated rows requires a re-embed (a mixed-model HNSW index is incoherent), and the new provider must be ZDR+DPA+EU-bound. Recorded on the `embedding_model` parameter note and here.
  6. **Idempotent offline pass.** `theygrow_api.embeddings_backfill` exposes `embed_backfill(...) -> EmbedSummary` and a `theygrow-embed` CLI (also `python -m …`) — offline batch, no `/api` endpoint (mirrors the M3 importer / M4-P1 derivation; ADR-005). Idempotency is **status-driven**: only `pending` (and, unless `--no-retry-failed`, `failed`) chunks are selected; `ready` rows are skipped, so a re-run over a fully-embedded corpus is a no-op. A provider error marks the batch `failed` (retried next run), never a partial write. BOTH `note` and `draft` chunks are embeddable (fork b stays open — no eligibility filter here).
  7. **Surface + seam reuse (M4-DL-002 forward requirement).** The P2 knobs land in the SAME config surface: `embedding_model` (runtime) + `embedding_dimension` (schema-bound), `changed_in=M4-DL-003`; `PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` bumps 1 → 2 (a knob-count change, not a value change). A new `EMBEDDING_COUNTERS` signal (counts + token cost + wall-clock) emits through the SAME `SignalSink` seam; §4-safe (token *count*, never text).
- **Rationale.** Lifting the adapter behind the pre-built port keeps M4 within ADR-005's transfer discipline and isolates the only permitted child-text egress to one ZDR-bound seam. Per-chunk storage matches the donor and lets RRF (P3) bolt on without reshaping; dropping the contradictory per-message shell keeps the schema honest. Freezing the dimension as a migration literal (with the drift guard as the surface↔schema link) reuses the exact pattern M4-DL-002 set for `fts_config`, so dimension changes are schema events, not silent edits. The fail-closed gate turns the ADR-011 §1 residency precondition into a structural guarantee — an unconfigured run cannot leak. Index-after-population and model-swap-means-re-embed are the owner's operational corrections, recorded so they are not re-litigated.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Thin httpx adapter instead of the donor SDK lift.** Rejected (owner choice) — that is closer to a rewrite than a lift; transferring the donor adapter with its `openai` dependency is the faithful ADR-005 §7 move.
  2. **Leave the dormant `source_messages.embedding` shell in place.** Rejected — it contradicts the final per-chunk granularity and is dead weight; dropping it in the new migration keeps immutability (a DROP in `0003`, not an edit to `0001`).
  3. **Build the HNSW index in migration `0003`.** Rejected (owner correction) — building on an all-NULL column then filling row-by-row degrades the graph; the index is a backfill step after population.
  4. **Gate only on endpoint/key presence (no clearance flag).** Rejected (owner correction) — presence is not consent; an explicit, default-false clearance flag makes ZDR+DPA+EU a structural §4 gate.
  5. **Treat `embedding_model` as a hot-swappable runtime knob.** Rejected — a model swap against populated rows yields a mixed-model index; "runtime" is swappable-with-backfill, recorded as such.
  6. **A separate `embedding_records` table (donor's literal shape).** Rejected — the reserved `event_chunks.embedding_status` discriminator + a per-chunk column on `event_chunks` is the theygrow form (M4-P1 reserved it); a new table widens the schema with no benefit at this granularity.
- **Supersedes.** None. Builds on **ADR-011** (the resolved owner fork: provider, dimension=1536, per-chunk granularity), `M4-DL-001` (the reserved `embedding_status` discriminator; the "Side-find for P2" granularity/dimension reconciliation now resolved here), `M4-DL-002` (the config surface + signal seam + migration-immutability/drift-guard discipline, reused), `M3-DL-002` (the reserved shell, now dropped), and **ADR-005** (engine out of perimeter; transfer-not-rewrite).
- **Effects.**
  - Adds `api/theygrow_api/adapters/embeddings/openai_client.py` (donor adapter lift), `api/theygrow_api/embeddings_backfill.py` (offline backfill + `theygrow-embed` CLI + fail-closed gate), and the `EmbeddingProvider` / `EmbeddingBatch` seam in `ports/provider.py`.
  - Schema: Alembic `0003_event_chunk_embeddings` (adds `event_chunks.embedding vector(1536)`; drops `source_messages.embedding`); `db/models.py` adds `EMBEDDING_DIMENSION` + the `EventChunk.embedding` column and removes `SourceMessage.embedding`.
  - Surface/seam: `parameters.py` gains `EMBEDDING_DIMENSION`, the `embedding_model` runtime knob, and the two `Parameter` rows (`PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` → 2); `signals.py` gains `EMBEDDING_COUNTERS` + `EmbeddingCounters`; `config.py` + `.env.example` gain the embedder endpoint/key/clearance fields.
  - Deps/scripts: `api/pyproject.toml` adds `openai>=1.0` and the `theygrow-embed` console entry.
  - Tests: adds `api/tests/test_embeddings_backfill.py` (DB-backed, injected fake provider — embed/idempotency/failed-retry/fork-b/signal/fail-closed) and `api/tests/test_embedding_schema.py`; extends `test_parameters.py` (dimension drift guard + the two knobs) and `test_signals.py` (the P2 stage + `EmbeddingCounters` §4 safety); updates `test_source_message_schema.py` / `test_import_export.py` for the dropped shell.
  - Enforced invariants `M4-P2-INV-001` (per-chunk vector is dimension-pinned + drift-guarded; dormant shell dropped), `M4-P2-INV-002` (backfill is fail-closed without the ZDR clearance gate — zero provider calls, zero writes), `M4-P2-INV-003` (backfill is idempotent + index-after-population). `M4-P1-INV-003` (signal §4-safety) now also covers `EMBEDDING_COUNTERS`.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled (M4-P1 **Done** @ 9a130e3; M4-P2 **Current**).
  - Out of this packet (deferred): RRF fusion (dense + sparse) + episodic-eligibility retrieval filter → **P3 [gated: fork b OPEN]**; grounded-ask assembly → **P4**; chat endpoint + nginx proxy → **M5**; the on-device embedder fallback (ADR-011 §1) unless the owner switches to it.

## M4-DL-004 — M4-P3 retrieval fusion + note-only episodic eligibility: dense leg, RRF, fail-closed query embedding, operability enforcement

- **Date.** 2026-06-25
- **Decision.** Close the retrieval seam by **transferring** the engine's dense leg + Reciprocal Rank Fusion (ADR-005 §7: lift, not rewrite — the donor `memory_rag.services.retrieval` is out of perimeter, so the RRF algorithm is transferred, NOT imported) under the two resolved owner forks (ADR-011, ADR-012), with these packet-level choices:
  1. **Dense leg mirrors the sparse leg.** `retrieval/search_repository.py` gains `dense_candidates(...)` — community-scoped cosine (`<=>`) ranking over `event_chunks.embedding` against the P2 HNSW index (`vector_cosine_ops`), only `embedding_status='ready'` rows, optional inclusive `note_date` range, one §4-safe `RETRIEVAL_LATENCY` emission. It is a **pure SQL port**: it takes an ALREADY-embedded `query_vector` — the provider call lives in the orchestrator, so the only child-text egress stays on the cleared embedder seam.
  2. **Note-only eligibility = hard WHERE in BOTH legs (ADR-012, fork b).** A binary, always-on FK join `JOIN source_messages sm ON sm.source_message_id = ec.source_message_id AND sm.detected_route='note'` on each leg — `draft` chunks are embedded + indexed (fork b kept them embeddable in P2) but DROPPED at retrieval, so no draft can reach a caller. NOT a tier/weight knob; `detected_route` stays on `source_messages` (not denormalized — the P1 decision held). The fused result inherits note-only by construction.
  3. **RRF in `services/retrieval.py`.** `reciprocal_rank_fusion(dense, sparse, k, dense_weight, sparse_weight)` fuses the two leg rankings: `score = Σ_leg weight_leg / (k + rank_leg)`, deduplicated by `chunk_id`, ordered by fused score DESC with a deterministic tiebreak (`created_at`, `event_index`, `chunk_id`). `retrieve(...)` is the orchestrator (embed query → both legs at `candidate_k` → fuse → truncate to `top_k`).
  4. **Unified `candidate_k` (owner-confirmed).** One runtime per-leg candidate cap the orchestrator passes to BOTH legs, so RRF fuses comparable-depth rankings. Precedence recorded: the orchestrator always passes `candidate_k`; the P1 `sparse_candidate_limit` (M4-DL-002) is the bare-leg default for DIRECT sparse calls only — the two never compete on the fused path. `sparse_candidate_limit` is NOT removed/renamed.
  5. **Fail-closed query embedding (owner correction, ADR-011 §1).** `retrieve()` embeds `query_text` — that is USER text leaving the perimeter to the third-party embedder, the exact surface ADR-011 governs — so it is NOT privacy-exempt for being read-path. The SAME ZDR+DPA+EU clearance gate as the offline backfill runs FIRST: an uncleared process raises `EmbedderNotReady` before any provider call or egress (zero provider calls), even with a provider injected. The gate (`_ensure_embedder_cleared`) and provider construction (`_build_provider`) are REUSED from `embeddings_backfill` — one fail-closed gate, never a second drifting copy.
  6. **`RETRIEVAL_LATENCY` extended with a `leg` label (owner-confirmed; reuse/extend, not duplicate).** A §4-safe `leg` discriminator (`"sparse"|"dense"|"fused"`) is added to the signal + taxonomy `field_names`; the P1 sparse emit now passes `leg="sparse"`. One signal kind, three distinguishable emissions; `leg` is a bounded label (counts/ids class), asserted §4-safe in `test_signals`.
  7. **`ef_search` deliberately DEFERRED (owner-confirmed).** No HNSW `ef_search` query knob in P3 — rely on the pgvector default; recall tuning is gated on a later metric (ADR-012 revisit trigger). Named here (not forgotten) on the `candidate_k` note so the later tuning step has an anchor.
  8. **Operability structural enforcement landed in P3 (owner-directed; closes ADR-013 OQ#1).** Two test-backed invariants make the operability surface self-guarding: `M4-P3-INV-001` (every config-surface parameter carries a non-empty `changed_in`) and `M4-P3-INV-003` (the `emitted_now=True` taxonomy set equals what producers actually emit, established by driving every producer through a recording sink). Plus `M4-P3-INV-002` (note-only eligibility enforced in both legs + the fused path). The config knobs land in the SAME surface (`candidate_k`, `top_k`, `rrf_k`, `rrf_dense_weight`, `rrf_sparse_weight`; all runtime; `changed_in=M4-DL-004`; `PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` 2 → 3, a knob-count change). No schema change, no migration.
- **Rationale.** Mirroring the sparse leg's shape keeps the dense leg a thin, testable SQL port and isolates the only query-text egress to the cleared embedder seam — the read path is held to the same §4 bar as the write path (an uncleared process cannot leak a query). Enforcing note-only as a hard WHERE in both legs (not a post-filter, not a weight) makes ADR-012's honest-degradation guarantee structural: draft data is unreachable by construction, so P4 assembly sees note-only candidates without trusting a caller to filter. A unified `candidate_k` keeps RRF fusing comparable depths from one knob. The `leg` extension reuses the one signal kind rather than fracturing the taxonomy. Folding the operability invariants into P3 turns ADR-013's "config + signals are traceable" intent into code gates — a knob without provenance or a `emitted_now` flag without a producer now fails CI.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Embed the query inside `dense_candidates`.** Rejected — that puts a provider call (and the §4 egress) inside the SQL port; keeping the embed in the orchestrator concentrates the gate at one seam and keeps the leg pure/injectable.
  2. **Apply the note-only filter once in the fusion layer (post-filter).** Rejected — a post-filter lets draft chunks ride in each leg's candidate window and only drops them after, wasting the `candidate_k` budget and risking a leak if a caller bypasses the orchestrator; a hard WHERE in each leg is the binary fork-b decision.
  3. **Separate `dense_candidate_limit` mirroring `sparse_candidate_limit`.** Rejected (owner choice) — two caps invite asymmetric depth and a fuzzy fused contract; one `candidate_k` matches the packet wording and single-sources the depth.
  4. **A new `RETRIEVAL_FUSION` signal kind for the fused timing.** Rejected (owner choice) — it leaves dense/sparse indistinguishable; extending `RETRIEVAL_LATENCY` with a `leg` label is the reuse the operability discipline asks for.
  5. **Introduce `ef_search` now.** Rejected (owner choice) — tuning a recall/latency knob before any recall measurement is premature; deferred and named for the metric-gated revisit.
  6. **Treat query embedding as privacy-exempt (read path).** Rejected (owner correction) — `query_text` is user text leaving the perimeter; the fail-closed gate must cover it exactly as it covers backfill.
- **Supersedes.** None. Builds on **ADR-011** (provider/dimension/per-chunk), **ADR-012** (note-only DROP at the retrieval filter), **ADR-013** (operability traceability — its OQ#1 closed by the two structural invariants), `M4-DL-001`/`M4-DL-002` (sparse leg + config surface/signal seam, reused/extended), `M4-DL-003` (the dense substrate + the fail-closed ZDR gate, reused), and **ADR-005** (engine out of perimeter; transfer-not-rewrite).
- **Effects.**
  - Adds `api/theygrow_api/services/retrieval.py` (RRF + the `retrieve` orchestrator with the reused fail-closed gate) and `api/theygrow_api/services/__init__.py`.
  - Extends `api/theygrow_api/retrieval/search_repository.py`: `dense_candidates(...)`, the shared `_row_to_candidate` helper, the note-only FK join on BOTH legs, and `leg`-labelled latency emissions.
  - Surface/seam: `parameters.py` gains the five P3 fusion knobs (`PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` → 3); `signals.py` extends `RetrievalLatency` + the `RETRIEVAL_LATENCY` taxonomy with the §4-safe `leg` label.
  - Tests: adds `api/tests/test_retrieval.py` (RRF purity + the orchestrator: fan-out/top_k/three-leg signals/end-to-end note-only/fail-closed-uncleared/empty-query) and `api/tests/test_signal_emitters.py` (the emitted-now ↔ producer coupling); extends `test_search_repository.py` (dense ordering/ready-filter/community-scope/short-circuit/`leg="dense"` + the both-legs draft-exclusion) and `test_parameters.py` (the five knobs + the non-empty-`changed_in` guard) and `test_signals.py` (the `leg` label safety). Full suite green against pgvector Postgres.
  - Enforced invariants `M4-P3-INV-001` (note-only eligibility in both legs + the fused path), `M4-P3-INV-002` (every parameter carries `changed_in`), `M4-P3-INV-003` (emitted_now kinds are wired to producers). `M4-P1-INV-003` (signal §4-safety) now also covers the `leg`-extended `RETRIEVAL_LATENCY`.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled (M4-P2 **Done** @ 5a7fe6f; M4-P3 **Current**).
  - Out of this packet (deferred): grounded-ask assembly (`context_assembler` + `query_service`) + the `GROUNDING_COVERAGE` / `DEGRADATION_EVENT` emitters → **P4** (defined-now, `emitted_now=False`); chat endpoint + nginx same-origin proxy → **M5**; the HNSW `ef_search` knob → later, metric-gated; the on-device embedder fallback (ADR-011 §1) unless the owner switches.

## M4-DL-005 — M4-P4 grounded-ask assembly: answers-provider lift, grounding gate, honest degradation, second-egress §4 gate, operability activation

- **Date.** 2026-06-25
- **Decision.** Close M4 by **transferring** the engine's grounded-ask seam (ADR-005 §7: lift, not rewrite — donor `memory_rag.services.{context_assembler,query_service}` + `core/domain/answer_prompt` + `core/domain/answer_schema` + `adapters/answers/openai_client`, all out of perimeter, transferred NOT imported) into a callable library seam (NO HTTP endpoint, NO chat UI — those are M5/ADR-007), under two NEW owner forks resolved this session:
  1. **ADR-014 — per-egress privacy clearance.** The answers/chat LLM is the SECOND third-party egress (the assembled family `chunk_text` goes to it) and a DISTINCT service/DPA/EU-residency surface from the embedder. It carries its OWN fail-closed clearance flag `answers_privacy_cleared` (+ `answers_base_url`/`answers_api_key` infra config, `answers_model` knob) — clearing the embedder does NOT clear answers. The gate (`_ensure_answers_cleared`) runs FIRST, before any egress, even with an injected provider: an uncleared process raises `AnswersNotReady` and makes ZERO provider calls. Mirrors the embeddings fail-closed gate; a separate gate, not a reuse, because it is a separate egress contract.
  2. **ADR-015 — honest degradation for grounded-but-uncertain.** A grounded answer the model flags `uncertain`/`ambiguous` is RETURNED WITH an honesty flag (`weak_evidence`/`ambiguous`) — it is still synthesized only from cited retrieved family context, never parametric, so it honors the closed corpus while being honest about confidence. Only no-context / empty-query / below-the-bar / provider-unavailable / parse-failure / fabricated-citation / model-declared `no_evidence` suppress the answer text and return an honest "no verified information" result.
  - **Grounding gate (new knob).** `grounding_min_segments` (runtime, default **1**) is the threshold: below it the service returns honest `no_evidence` with ZERO provider calls. COUNT-based, not score-based — RRF fuses on rank, not calibrated scores (score calibration is out of scope, M4-DL-004), so a score threshold would be meaningless across queries.
  - **Closed-corpus enforcement is structural on three edges:** the prompt carries only retrieved `chunk_text`; `parse_structured_answer` rejects any citation absent from the assembled context (fabricated citation → `parse_failure`); the pre-provider grounding gate refuses below the bar. The only grounded source at M4 is family episodic memory — canon/KB (174 skill descriptions) is M5, out of perimeter; provenance is family-observation lineage only (no canon source introduced).
  - **Operability activation (ADR-013 / contract §4.7).** The two deferred signals land their producers and flip `emitted_now=True`: `GROUNDING_COVERAGE` (`covered`/`total` cited-vs-offered counts) and `DEGRADATION_EVENT` (a §4-safe bounded `mode` label). INV-003's coupling test now DRIVES `answer_query` and REQUIRES their real emission. New knobs land in the same config surface (`answers_model`, `grounding_min_segments`; both runtime; `changed_in=M4-DL-005`; `PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` 3 → 4, a knob-count change). No schema change, no migration.
  - **Donor availability.** As of this packet the donor clone IS on disk (`/home/dev/telegram-dairy/src/memory_rag`), so P4 is a faithful transfer of the donor algorithm. NOTE: M4-P2/P3 were transfer-IN-SHAPE reconstructions (the donor was not on disk then), not byte-faithful file lifts; that discrepancy is to be reconciled in the repo-bridge at milestone close. ADR-005 §7 holds either way — no `memory_rag` import in `api/theygrow_api/`.
- **Rationale.** Treating answer synthesis as a second, independently-cleared egress (ADR-014) keeps the §4 surface honest: a cleared embedder must not silently authorize a different vendor's chat model to receive child text. Making honest degradation structural on three edges (closed prompt + citation grounding + pre-provider gate) means "no parametric/web fallback" is enforced by construction, not by trusting the model — the strongest edge is the parser rejecting any citation outside the retrieved set. Returning grounded-but-uncertain answers with a flag (ADR-015) preserves usefulness without breaking the closed corpus, since such answers are still cited to retrieved family memory. Activating the two operability signals turns ADR-013's intent into a CI gate that now fails if a P4 producer stops emitting. Keeping this a library seam (no endpoint) makes M4 demoable end-to-end while leaving the transport for M5.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Reuse `embedder_privacy_cleared` to gate answers (one flag).** Rejected (owner, ADR-014) — the chat model is a distinct DPA/residency contract; one flag would let embedder clearance authorize an uncleared answers vendor. Separate flag, fail-closed.
  2. **Collapse grounded-but-uncertain to "no verified information".** Rejected (owner, ADR-015) — an uncertain answer cited to retrieved family memory is still closed-corpus; suppressing it discards useful, honest information. Return it with a flag.
  3. **Score-based grounding threshold (min fused RRF score).** Rejected — RRF scores are uncalibrated across queries (M4-DL-004 defers calibration); a count floor is the only sound, query-independent bar.
  4. **A new signal kind per degradation mode.** Rejected — one `DEGRADATION_EVENT` kind with a bounded `mode` label mirrors the `RETRIEVAL_LATENCY` `leg` reuse discipline (M4-DL-004), not a fractured taxonomy.
  5. **Lift the donor's resilience/retry layer for the answers adapter.** Rejected (scope) — not present in the P2 embeddings adapter either; a provider failure grades to one `provider_unavailable` degradation (no retry/repair). Deferred.
  6. **Persist queries / answer traces (donor `Query`/`AnswerTrace`/`RetrievalHit`).** Rejected (scope) — this is a library seam; theygrow has no such tables and this packet adds no migration.
- **Supersedes.** None. Introduces **ADR-014** (per-egress privacy clearance) and **ADR-015** (honest degradation for grounded-but-uncertain). Builds on **ADR-005** (engine out of perimeter; transfer-not-rewrite), **ADR-011** (embedder provider/clearance — the answers gate mirrors its fail-closed shape), **ADR-012** (note-only eligibility — P4 assembly sees note-only candidates by construction), **ADR-013** (operability traceability — the two signals activated), and `M4-DL-001`…`M4-DL-004`.
- **Effects.**
  - Adds `api/theygrow_api/services/context_assembler.py` (pure: `assemble_context`, the closed-corpus `build_answer_prompt`/`PROMPT_VERSION` + cross-community guard, `parse_structured_answer` citation grounding) and `api/theygrow_api/services/query_service.py` (`answer_query` orchestrator + `AnswersNotReady` / `_ensure_answers_cleared` / `_build_answers_provider` + `GroundedAnswer` / `SegmentProvenance`).
  - Adds `api/theygrow_api/adapters/answers/{__init__,openai_client}.py` (`OpenAIAnswersProvider` + `AnswersProviderUnavailable`; lazy `openai` import, no resilience layer).
  - Extends `ports/provider.py` (`AnswersProvider` Protocol + `AnswerResponse`); `config.py` (`answers_base_url`/`answers_api_key`/`answers_privacy_cleared`); `parameters.py` (`answers_model`, `grounding_min_segments`; `PARAMETERS_SCHEMA_VERSION` → 4); `signals.py` (`GroundingCoverage`/`DegradationEvent` dataclasses; both kinds `emitted_now=True`).
  - Tests: adds `api/tests/test_query_service.py` (success-with-provenance, answers fail-closed zero-calls, no-context degradation, present-but-irrelevant model-declared `no_evidence`, fabricated-citation parse-failure, provider-unavailable, uncertain-but-grounded-with-flag, empty-query, missing-community); extends `test_signal_emitters.py` (drives `answer_query`, the defined-not-emitted set now empty), `test_signals.py` (the two new payloads + `mode` label safety, taxonomy all-emitted), `test_provider_port.py` (`AnswersProvider` conformance), `test_parameters.py` (schema v4 + the two knobs).
  - Enforced invariants `M4-P4-INV-001` (answer synthesis fail-closed without `answers_privacy_cleared`), `M4-P4-INV-002` (grounded answers are closed-corpus or honestly degraded). `M4-P3-INV-003` extended to require the two P4 emissions.
  - Validation scope: the new tests prove SEAM MECHANICS with injected fakes — NOT retrieval recall, Russian-FTS adequacy (`simple`, M4-DL-001), or real-LLM `no_evidence` self-declaration. That recall/grounding-QUALITY mini-eval is the **M4-CLOSE** step against the real corpus with cleared real providers.
  - Out of this packet (deferred): chat endpoint `/api/chat` + PWA chat screen + nginx same-origin proxy → **M5** (ADR-007); canon/KB grounded source + medical-boundary red-flag handling → **M5**; answers retry/resilience, query/answer-trace persistence, `ef_search`, on-device embedder fallback → later, demand-gated.

## M4-DL-006 — M4-P5 (M4-CLOSE): repo-bridge lift-fidelity reconciliation + docs reconciliation

- **Date.** 2026-06-25
- **Type.** Docs + verification (read-only over the lifted seams; no product-code, schema, or migration change). Discharges the two milestone-close obligations that the functional packets P1–P4 left open: the **faithful-lift reconciliation** that `M4-DL-005` explicitly parked "at milestone close" (the donor was off-disk during P2/P3, so those were transfer-IN-SHAPE reconstructions) and the **docs reconciliation** of `docs/execution-map.md`. The lifted seams are frozen as shipped; this packet only verifies and records.
- **Decision.** With the donor clone now on disk (`/home/dev/telegram-dairy/src/memory_rag`, read out-of-perimeter under ADR-005 §7 — no import, no runtime edge), diff all seven lifted seams against it and record a per-seam fidelity disposition; reconcile the state map to milestone close; record the M4-CLOSE recall/grounding-quality mini-eval as recorded-NOT-run. **Finding: every divergence is covered by a prior decision — ZERO behavior-relevant divergence without a covering decision.** Per-seam dispositions:
  1. **P1 parser** (`domain/parser.py` ⟷ `core/domain/parser.py`) — **confirmed-faithful.** The algorithm body (`_split_non_empty_lines` / `_parse_iso_date` / `parse_note`, the `ParsedNote` dataclass) is byte-identical; only the module docstring is expanded to record the `created_at`-fallback divergence (`M4-DL-001`, where the engine instead surfaced `INVALID_INPUT`). No behavior change.
  2. **P2 embeddings adapter** (`adapters/embeddings/openai_client.py`) — **noted-deliberate-divergence.** The core call (`embeddings.create(model, input, dimensions=…)`) is faithful. Deltas, all covered by `M4-DL-003`: class rename `OpenAIEmbeddingClient` → `OpenAIEmbeddingProvider`; the donor resilience/retry layer (`adapters.resilience.run_with_retries`) is NOT lifted; returns an `EmbeddingBatch` (vectors + `total_tokens`) vs the donor's bare `list[list[float]]` — the token usage feeds the `EMBEDDING_COUNTERS` signal; a defensive sort-by-`index` is added. The donor's post-call length/dimension assertions are dropped — a behavior-neutral hardening reduction (consistent with "resilience layer not lifted"), backstopped by the `event_chunks.embedding vector(1536)` column type rejecting any wrong-dimension write and the backfill status-machine marking the batch `failed`.
  3. **P3 sparse leg** (`retrieval/search_repository.py` `sparse_candidates` ⟷ donor SQL in `storage/postgres/store.py`; the donor `storage/search_repository.py` is a Protocol contract only, so fidelity was checked against the algorithm-bearing SQL per the P5 plan) — **confirmed-faithful + recorded theygrow addition.** The `websearch_to_tsquery('simple', …) @@ chunk_text_tsv` match, the `ts_rank_cd(…) DESC, created_at, event_index` ordering, the community scope, and the inclusive `note_date` range are identical. The added `JOIN source_messages … detected_route='note'` is the note-only eligibility filter (ADR-012 / `M4-DL-004`) — the donor predates fork b, so this is an intended divergence, not infidelity.
  4. **P3 dense leg** (`dense_candidates` ⟷ donor `store.py.dense_candidates`) — **noted-deliberate-divergence.** The cosine `<=>` operator, nearest-first ascending order, and `embedding_status='ready'` guard are faithful. Deltas: the per-chunk `event_chunks.embedding` column replaces the donor's separate `embedding_records er` join (ADR-011 §3 / `M4-DL-003` — per-chunk granularity; the separate table was explicitly rejected); the donor's `model_name` filter is dropped (single-model corpus; model-swap-means-re-embed, `M4-DL-003`); the note-only FK join is added (ADR-012); a `created_at, event_index` secondary tiebreak is added (the donor dense leg ordered on cosine only) — a behavior-neutral determinism touch that mirrors the sparse leg's existing tiebreak and the RRF deterministic-tiebreak ethos (`M4-DL-004`), affecting only exact-distance ties.
  5. **P3 RRF** (`services/retrieval.py` ⟷ `services/retrieval.py`) — **noted-deliberate-divergence; both behavior-relevant deltas explicitly recorded in `M4-DL-004` §3.** (a) **Weighted** RRF (`Σ weight_leg / (k + rank)`, per-leg `rrf_dense_weight` / `rrf_sparse_weight` knobs) vs the donor's **unweighted** `Σ 1/(k + rank)`; (b) a **deterministic content tiebreak** (`created_at, event_index, chunk_id`) vs the donor's **first-appearance-order** tiebreak. Also `FusedHit` → `FusedCandidate` (carries per-leg ranks for P4 provenance) and the explicit two-leg signature + the `retrieve()` orchestrator (theygrow additions). The score formula and dedup-by-`chunk_id` are faithful. These are exactly the kind of behavior divergence the P5 decision rule targets — surfaced, and confirmed covered (no escalation).
  6. **P4 assembly trio** (`services/context_assembler.py` ⟷ donor `services/context_assembler.py` + `core/domain/answer_prompt.py` + `core/domain/answer_schema.py`, three donor files consolidated inline) — **confirmed-faithful with two covered prompt-text deltas.** `parse_structured_answer` is byte-faithful (identical shape validation, fabricated-citation gate, and the `no_evidence`-only-empty-citations rule; modulo `AnswerContext` → `AssembledContext`); `assemble_context` drops the donor's `Query` / `model_name` / `created_at` fields (library-only, no persistence). `build_answer_prompt` is faithful with two text deltas: the system prompt now advertises the `"ambiguous"` marker (the donor advertised only 3 of the 4 markers its own parser already accepted; surfacing `ambiguous` aligns with ADR-015 honest degradation) and "diary" → "family-diary" (product framing). `PROMPT_VERSION` stays `"v1"` — theygrow's own first prompt version.
  7. **P4 answers adapter** (`adapters/answers/openai_client.py`) — **noted-deliberate-divergence.** The Slice-4.5 call contour (`chat.completions.create`, `response_format={"type":"json_object"}`, `temperature=0`, `(openai.OpenAIError, TimeoutError)` translation) is faithful. Deltas, covered by `M4-DL-005`: class rename `OpenAIChatClient` → `OpenAIAnswersProvider`; the donor resilience/retry + `latency_ms` measurement are NOT lifted (a provider failure grades to one `provider_unavailable`); exception type `ChatProviderUnavailableError` → `AnswersProviderUnavailable`; returns `AnswerResponse(raw_text, total_tokens)` vs the donor's `ChatResponse(…, token_counts, latency_ms)`.
  8. **P4 query_service** (`services/query_service.py` ⟷ `services/query_service.py`) — **noted-deliberate-divergence (the large reduction), orchestration + grading faithful.** The persistence layer (`Query` / `AnswerTrace` / `RetrievalHit` writes, `_persist_*`, the `_per_leg_score` trace scoring) is NOT lifted (library seam, `M4-DL-005` alt #6); the donor's `FallbackMode` enum is replaced by §4-safe bounded string `mode` labels emitted via the `DEGRADATION_EVENT` + `GROUNDING_COVERAGE` signals (operability, `M4-DL-005`); `grounding_min_segments` parameterizes the grounding bar (`M4-DL-005`). The orchestration contour and the marker→mode grading are faithful: the lifted `_MARKER_TO_MODE` (`confident→None, uncertain→weak_evidence, ambiguous→ambiguous, no_evidence→no_evidence`) is semantically identical to the donor `_MARKER_TO_FALLBACK`; the degradation contours (empty-query, below-bar, provider-unavailable, parse-failure incl. fabricated-citation, LLM-declared `no_evidence` suppress; `uncertain`/`ambiguous` return-with-flag) match. The two fail-closed §4 gates (answers-clearance first; embedder-clearance inside `retrieve`) are theygrow's ADR-014 / ADR-011 additions.
  - **Perimeter (ADR-005 §7).** A grep over `api/theygrow_api/` finds ZERO `memory_rag` / `diary_rag` imports — only docstring/comment references naming the donor. The engine remained a code donor, never a runtime edge.
  - **M4-CLOSE mini-eval — recorded, NOT run.** The recall / grounding-quality mini-eval is an owner-run step against the REAL corpus with cleared real providers (`embedder_privacy_cleared` + `answers_privacy_cleared`), out of this docs packet. It measures (1) lexical + dense retrieval recall on the Russian corpus and (2) grounded-answer quality / honest-degradation correctness with a real answers LLM. It gates two named port-out triggers: the ADR-012 draft-share / note-only-eligibility revisit, and the `'simple'`-FTS → `'russian'` / ParadeDB Russian-recall trigger (`M4-DL-001`). The P1–P4 tests prove SEAM MECHANICS with injected fakes — not recall or grounding quality; that distinction is unchanged by this packet.
- **Rationale.** The faithful-lift reconciliation is owed because P2/P3 were transfer-IN-SHAPE while the donor was off-disk (`M4-DL-005`); now that it is present, diffing against the algorithm-bearing source (the SQL in `store.py`, not the donor Protocol) is what proves BEHAVIOR fidelity rather than mere signature shape. Recording each divergence against its covering decision turns "is this a faithful lift?" into an auditable per-seam answer and confirms the milestone introduced no silent rewrite. Reconciling the state map closes the gap where it still showed P3 as the current packet with no P4/P5 line. Keeping the mini-eval recorded-not-run preserves the validation-honesty boundary: this packet certifies fidelity + the green gate, not corpus quality.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **PR M4 without the fidelity reconciliation.** Rejected — `M4-DL-005` explicitly parked it "at milestone close"; shipping with the milestone's own log flagging an unreconciled lift is incoherent.
  2. **Diff against the donor `storage/search_repository.py` Protocol only (contract-level).** Rejected (owner direction) — the Protocol carries no behavior; behavioral drift lives in the concrete SQL, so the targeted spot-check of `storage/postgres/store.py` is required. A full line-diff of `store.py` was also rejected as out of scope (it audits non-behavioral connection/cursor infra).
  3. **Rewrite the two un-decision-cited micro-touches (dropped embedding length assertions; added dense determinism tiebreak) to match the donor.** Rejected — both are behavior-neutral and this is a frozen-seam docs packet; recording them is the correct disposition, not a rewrite. Had any divergence been behavior-relevant without a covering decision, the rule was to surface it for an owner decision, not fold a fix in here.
  4. **Mark the milestone Done only after the mini-eval.** Rejected — the mini-eval is an owner-run operational step against cleared real providers, not a code packet; it is not a PR-blocking stop-condition clause.
- **Supersedes.** None. Builds on **ADR-005** §7 (engine out of perimeter; transfer-not-rewrite), **ADR-011** / **ADR-012** / **ADR-014** / **ADR-015** (the resolved owner forks the seams implement), and `M4-DL-001`…`M4-DL-005` (the packet decisions each divergence is recorded against). Reads the donor at `/home/dev/telegram-dairy/src/memory_rag`.
- **Effects.**
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled to milestone close: M4 marked **Done**; P3 **Done** @ db9f830; the **P4** line added (**Done** @ 5f01d02, ruff-format fixup @ fddfc27); the **P5 (M4-CLOSE)** line added (this packet); M5 advanced to next milestone.
  - This entry (`M4-DL-006`) records the eight per-seam fidelity dispositions and the mini-eval disposition. No new enforced invariant (so `docs/INVARIANTS.md` is untouched), no parameter / signal change, no `api/` edit, no schema, no migration.
  - Gate recorded green at this packet: `ruff@0.9.2 format --check` (49 files formatted) + `ruff@0.9.2 check` (all checks passed); `mypy@1.14.1 --config-file=pyproject.toml api` (strict — no issues, 45 source files); `pytest api` (104 passed, DB-backed against `pgvector/pgvector:pg16`); `scripts/check-contract-integrity.sh` (OK, with the edited `execution-map.md` in the scanned corpus); the perimeter grep (zero `memory_rag` imports). Note: the repo-of-record pins `ruff` v0.9.2 — a stale standalone `ruff` 0.2.1 reports false stub-formatting drift; the pinned version is clean.
  - Out of this packet: the M4-CLOSE recall/grounding mini-eval (owner-run, cleared real providers); the vault repo-bridge re-revision (orchestrator/vault, post-merge cadence); everything M5 (chat endpoint + nginx; canon/KB + medical boundary); the `ef_search` and FTS → `'russian'` metric-gated port-outs.

## B1-DL-018 — Б1 ZPD filter (= vault ADR-018): readiness as pure prerequisite graph; age demoted; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-25
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen Б1 functional ladder (P1–P4); no product-code, schema, or migration change. The ZPD filter is frozen as shipped; this packet only records and reconciles. *(Id note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `B1-DL-018` (the Б-series frontend track) is a deliberate, owner-pinned one-off — it matches the four shipped commit trailers. The id is pinned to the trailers, not renumbered; the format header is intentionally left unedited this packet.)*
- **Decision.** Define the ZPD (zone-of-proximal-development) readiness decision as shipped across the Б1 `/app` skills-tracker ladder, recorded here as repo decision-log id `B1-DL-018` (= vault **ADR-018**):
  1. **Readiness = pure prerequisite graph.** A skill is *ready* iff `not-completed ∧ all prerequisites completed` — **no age gate** (P1 `62a07d6`: the readiness predicate + presentational row markers, the presentational core).
  2. **Age demoted to a passive display reference** — age is no longer a filter input.
  3. **The active age filter is replaced by the ZPD filter** (P2 `e2ecf60`: toggle + `applyFilter`).
  4. **Honest empty-state** — when the ZPD filter is on and no skill is ready, the tracker shows an honest message, not an empty table (P3 `51cc9e2`; cites `ADR-018 / контракт §2`, matching `app/index.html:2256`).
  5. **Onboarding copy reconciled** to the ZPD filter (P4 `79b6b28`).
  - **Cross-link (explicit).** Vault **ADR-018** is the upstream decision. The in-code `ADR-018` citations (`app/index.html:1566, 1819, 2101, 2256`) resolve to it, and this entry is the **repo-side definition site** the four `B1-DL-018` commit trailers cite — so both citation forms (`ADR-018` in code, `B1-DL-018` in trailers) now resolve transitively.
- **Rationale.** Readiness is a property of the prerequisite graph, not of age; gating on age conflated "old enough" with "prerequisites met". Demoting age to display and replacing the age filter makes the ready-zone a pure ZPD lens — the skills a child is positioned to learn next. The honest empty-state keeps the filter truthful when the ready-zone is empty (no silent empty table that reads as "nothing exists"). Recording the decision in the repo log closes the gap where the shipped commit trailers and the in-code `ADR-018` comments cited records that did not resolve in the repo.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Renumber to `B1-DL-001`.** Rejected (owner) — the four shipped trailers cite `B1-DL-018`; renumbering would desync the log from the trailers or force a git-history edit. The id is pinned to the trailers.
  2. **Keep age as a secondary gate alongside prerequisites.** Rejected — readiness is a pure prerequisite-graph property; an age co-gate would hide ready skills and reintroduce the conflation the milestone removed. Age is display-only.
  3. **Empty table when no skill is ready.** Rejected — dishonest; P3's explicit empty-state message is the honest-degradation form (`ADR-018 / контракт §2`).
- **Supersedes.** None in this log — the `/app` age filter predates this decision-log (a backend M-series artifact) and was never a logged decision. The ZPD filter **replaces** that prior product behavior (active age filter → ZPD filter; age → passive display).
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** `62a07d6` (ZPD readiness predicate + presentational row markers), **P2** `e2ecf60` (ZPD filter replaces the age filter — toggle + `applyFilter`), **P3** `51cc9e2` (honest empty-state for the empty ready-zone), **P4** `79b6b28` (onboarding copy reconciled to the ZPD filter).
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: the `Б1 — Done` block added (P1–P4 + this B1-CLOSE close-out), and the `M5` line reconciled to deferred-per-ADR-017 (Б1 was sequenced ahead of M5; the next milestone is an open owner decision).
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — Б1 added no enforced test / lint / schema / runtime guard (enforced-only; nothing is owed).
  - **No product-code / schema / migration change** in this packet — the ZPD filter is frozen as shipped (`app/index.html`).
  - Cross-links vault **ADR-018** (the upstream decision; the definition site for the in-code `ADR-018` citations). This entry does **not** reference its own (future) commit SHA — the close-out lands "this packet".

## PWA-DL-001 — PWA Update-Delivery: network-first SW delivery + prompt-driven update UX; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-26
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen P1–P2 functional ladder; no product-code / schema / migration change this packet. The SW delivery engine and the update UX are frozen as shipped; this entry only records and reconciles. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `PWA-DL-001` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **delivery** track (roadmap v3 §5 contour 7) — it is **not** track-Б (`Б-активности` / `B1-DL-018` is the separate track-Б sibling) and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. It is a fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to the vault ADR it cross-references. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet.)*
- **Decision.** Record the PWA Update-Delivery milestone as shipped across `app/sw.js` (P1) + `app/index.html` (P2):
  1. **Cache strategy = network-first app shell** (not versioned-precache). `sw.js` serves navigation / app-shell and `/api/` **network-first** — existing installs get the freshly deployed `index.html` on the next navigation, no hard-refresh — and **cache-first** only for genuinely static assets. The successful navigation response is mirrored into the cache keyed to `'/'` so the offline-fallback copy stays current.
  2. **`CACHE_VERSION` single-source-of-truth knob.** One front-side constant (`app/sw.js`, currently `'v2'`) drives the cache generation; bumping it ships a fresh app shell, and `activate()` purges every non-current cache (a one-time migration off `theygrow-v1`). A **typed** knob is deliberately deferred — the typed-config home (`parameters.py`) lives in `/api`, untouched this front-only milestone — so a front-side literal is the justified bounded form.
  3. **Owner UX decision = prompt / banner, not auto-apply.** A new waiting worker surfaces the in-app `#updateBanner` "Обновить" prompt; the user drives activation. Auto-apply was rejected — it can swap the app under an active session.
  4. **SW neutrality to the UX policy (per-edge).** `install` does **no** `skipWaiting()` — the new worker parks in `waiting`; the only path to `skipWaiting` is the `SKIP_WAITING` message handler, posted by P2 when the user accepts. `activate()`'s `clients.claim()` only claims after activation (first-install-silent) and does not force activation. In `index.html` the reload is **double-gated**: `updateAccepted` (set only on the user tap, so the first-install `controllerchange` from `clients.claim()` does **not** reload) plus `refreshing` (one-shot — exactly one reload, no loop).
  5. **Id resolution + deferred physical backfill (authoritative here).** The `app/sw.js` `changed_in: <Б-DL-NNN, assigned in P3>` placeholder is **resolved to `PWA-DL-001` by this entry**. The physical sw.js comment edit is **deliberately deferred** to the next natural `CACHE_VERSION` bump: browsers detect SW updates by byte-comparing `/sw.js` (served `no-cache`), so a comment-only edit now would register as a "new" worker on every install and surface a **spurious no-op update prompt** (the banner with no real shell change, since `CACHE_VERSION` is unchanged). The id is therefore authoritative in this log now; the comment string is corrected when sw.js next legitimately changes.
- **Rationale.** Network-first shell reaches "fresh shell on next navigation, no hard-refresh" with **no precache manifest and no build toolchain** — the decisive reason versioned-precache was rejected (it needs a bundler / manifest step the single-file PWA deliberately avoids). Keeping `skipWaiting` behind the message handler keeps the SW neutral to the UX policy, so the prompt-vs-auto-apply decision lives entirely in `index.html`. Double-gating the reload prevents both the first-install reload and multi-reload loops. Deferring the physical sw.js backfill avoids paying a user-visible no-op prompt for a documentation correction; the decision-log is the authoritative id home.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Versioned-precache (workbox-style manifest).** Rejected — requires a build toolchain / bundler the single-file PWA avoids; network-first shell reaches the same freshness with none.
  2. **Auto-apply the update (no prompt).** Rejected (owner) — swaps the app under an active session; an explicit banner is the chosen UX.
  3. **`skipWaiting()` in `install` (immediate activation).** Rejected — couples the SW to an auto-apply policy and bypasses the prompt; the message-handler path keeps the SW neutral.
  4. **Backfill the sw.js `changed_in` comment physically this packet.** Rejected — a byte change to `/sw.js` (served `no-cache`) triggers the update flow on every install → spurious no-op prompt; resolve the id in the log, backfill the comment at the next `CACHE_VERSION` bump.
  5. **A typed `CACHE_VERSION` knob now.** Rejected — the typed-config surface is `/api` `parameters.py`, out of this front-only milestone; a front-side literal is the bounded form.
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `PWA-DL` namespace. Cross-references vault **ADR-019** (the upstream owner decision for prompt-driven update delivery; this entry is the repo-side definition site, mirroring how `B1-DL-018` cross-references vault ADR-018). Builds on the M2-P1 monorepo split (PWA under `/app`) and the M2-P3 build topology (`app/` build-config + nginx).
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** `107de2a` (network-first SW delivery engine — versioned cache + `skipWaiting` handler), **P2** `273c9ab` (prompt-driven update UX driving SW skip-waiting; first-install-silent, single guarded reload).
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `PWA Update-Delivery — Done` sibling block added (P1/P2 + this PWA-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 block and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine (cross-cutting delivery track, roadmap §5 contour 7 — not naively "next" in that ladder).
  - `docs/RUNBOOK.md` "What runs today" notes the network-first app-shell delivery + prompt-driven update; the `/sw.js` `no-cache, must-revalidate` cache surface is confirmed to live in `app/nginx.conf` (`app/cloudbuild.yaml` carries **no** cache directives — the planning-brief's cloudbuild mention was moot).
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — the milestone added no enforced test / lint / schema / runtime guard; `/app` is unguarded (`M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it). "Network-first shell" / "prompt-driven skipWaiting" are runtime behaviour, not machine-enforced (Б1 precedent: no INVARIANTS.md entry).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 restatements; update-delivery is delivery mechanics, not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `app/` change** this packet — the SW engine + update UX are frozen as shipped; the `app/sw.js` `changed_in` comment is resolved to `PWA-DL-001` here and physically backfilled at the next `CACHE_VERSION` bump.

## L1-DL-001 — L1 deploy-safety promotion gate (= vault ADR-020): no-traffic + sha-tag deploy, owner-smoke promotion; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-26
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen L1 P1–P2 ladder; no product-code / schema / migration change this packet (the P1 build-config change shipped in L1-P1). The promotion gate is frozen as shipped; this entry only records and reconciles. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `L1-DL-001` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting deploy-safety track — it is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. It is a fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to the vault ADR it cross-references. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring `PWA-DL-001` / `B1-DL-018`.)*
- **Decision.** Record the L1 deploy-safety promotion gate as shipped across `app/cloudbuild.yaml` (P1) + `docs/RUNBOOK.md` (P2):
  1. **No-traffic + sha-tag deploy.** `app/cloudbuild.yaml` Step 3 deploys the PWA with `--no-traffic --tag sha-$SHORT_SHA`, so every push lands a fresh revision at **0% traffic**, reachable only via its **per-revision** `sha-<SHORT_SHA>` tagged URL. The live revision keeps serving untouched until the owner promotes — a bad build can no longer reach users merely by deploying.
  2. **Owner-smoke promotion gate, not CI.** Promotion is an **owner-gated manual step**: the owner smoke-tests the just-deployed tagged revision (health + app-shell DOM + update-banner presence + the `/sw.js` `no-cache` header — a `curl`-only checklist, no JS toolchain) and then shifts 100% traffic to it. The gate is **owner smoke, not a CI check**; Claude Code does not run `gcloud`. The full procedure (get the tagged URL → smoke → promote → rollback/abort → tag pruning) lives in `docs/RUNBOOK.md` ("Promotion + rollback").
  3. **Abort = withhold; rollback = pin previous revision.** Aborting needs no action — withholding promotion leaves prod on the prior revision (the no-traffic revision serves no users). Rolling back after promotion pins traffic to the previous good revision. Both are owner `gcloud` actions recorded in RUNBOOK.
  4. **Tag accumulation pruned out-of-band.** Promotion does not remove the `sha-` tag, so tags accumulate harmlessly (each routes 0% traffic to an old revision); pruning is an out-of-band owner action — no automated pruning this milestone.
  5. **Live-infra names stay in RUNBOOK.** The PWA Cloud Run service and region are live-infra identifiers; per the contract-integrity gate (`M1-P3-INV-002`) they are confined to `docs/RUNBOOK.md`, the sole carrier. This entry and `docs/execution-map.md` name only the build-config file path (`app/cloudbuild.yaml`) and link to RUNBOOK for the identifiers.
- **Rationale.** A plain `gcloud run deploy` shifts 100% traffic on success, so a green-but-broken build reaches every user the moment it deploys. `--no-traffic --tag sha-$SHORT_SHA` decouples *deploy* from *release*: the revision is built and individually addressable for smoke, but serves no users until an explicit owner promotion — turning "deploy" into a safe, reversible staging step **without standing up a separate staging service** (none exists; ADR-020). Keeping the gate owner-smoke rather than CI matches the reality that the smoke is a human judgement against a live tagged URL (and that Claude Code does not run `gcloud`); a curl-only checklist keeps it toolchain-free for the single-file PWA. Confining the live-infra names to RUNBOOK keeps the contract + spine files name-clean (`M1-P3-INV-002`).
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Plain 100%-traffic deploy (status quo before L1).** Rejected — a successful build is released to all users immediately; there is no gap to smoke a bad shell before it serves.
  2. **A standalone staging Cloud Run service.** Rejected (ADR-020) — a second service duplicates infra/config for what per-revision `--no-traffic --tag` already gives: an addressable, zero-traffic revision of the *same* service to smoke and promote.
  3. **A CI smoke gate instead of owner smoke.** Rejected — the smoke is a human check against a live tagged URL; Claude Code does not run `gcloud`, and the single-file PWA has no JS test toolchain. The owner curl-checklist is the bounded form.
  4. **Auto-prune `sha-` tags on promotion.** Rejected — premature; accumulated tags are harmless (0% traffic to old revisions); pruning stays an out-of-band owner action.
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `L1-DL` namespace. Cross-references vault **ADR-020** (the upstream owner decision for the deploy-safety promotion gate; this entry is the repo-side definition site, mirroring how `PWA-DL-001` cross-references vault ADR-019 and `B1-DL-018` cross-references vault ADR-018). Builds on the M2-P3 build topology (`app/cloudbuild.yaml` + its path-filtered trigger) and the PWA Update-Delivery delivery surface (the smoke checklist asserts the network-first shell + `#updateBanner` + the `/sw.js` `no-cache` header from `PWA-DL-001`).
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** `f0cf7c9` (`app/cloudbuild.yaml` Step 3 → `--no-traffic --tag sha-$SHORT_SHA`), **P2** `e76de87` (`docs/RUNBOOK.md` promotion-gate procedure + owner smoke checklist).
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: an `L1 Promotion Gate — Done` sibling block added (P1/P2 + this L1-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 and PWA Update-Delivery blocks and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine (cross-cutting deploy-safety track — not naively "next" in that ladder).
  - **Piggybacked owed cleanup (M2-P3, gated and now satisfied):** the now-historical "Build-config relocation (M2-P3) — trigger continuity" caveat block is removed from `docs/RUNBOOK.md`. Its forward instruction ("the PWA trigger MUST be repointed before/atomically with the merge") is discharged — the relocation happened and the two-trigger steady state is live (owner-applied, confirmed green). The historical `M2-DL-002` decision-log entry is left untouched (a historical artifact); only the live RUNBOOK caveat is cleaned.
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — L1 adds an owner-gated operational *procedure*, not a machine-enforced test / lint / schema / runtime guard; the gate is owner smoke, not CI (precedent: `PWA-DL-001` / `B1-DL-018` added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; a deploy-promotion gate is delivery/ops mechanics, not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `app/` change** this packet — the build-config change shipped in L1-P1; the live-infra identifiers stay confined to `docs/RUNBOOK.md` (`M1-P3-INV-002`).

## B2-DL-001 — Б2 Defect A diagnosis (= vault ADR-018): activities visibility is pure prerequisite graph; honest-empty-state already holds; no-fix

- **Date.** 2026-06-27
- **Type.** Verification / diagnosis record (read-only — no product-code, copy, schema, or migration change; this is the Б2 P1 read-only diagnosis, which made **no edit and no commit**). Records the owner-disposed finding for **Defect A** in the ZPD/activities-correctness milestone (track Б, sibling of Б1). *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `B2-DL` is the dedicated namespace for the **second track-Б milestone** (ZPD activities-list correctness — commit prefix `zpd-b`), the sibling of `B1-DL-018`. Unlike `B1-DL-018` — which was number-pinned to `018` only because four already-shipped commit trailers cited it — Б2's shipped P2/P3 trailers (`689f30e` / `d2652b4`) cite **no DL-id**, so nothing forces an ADR-number pin. `B2-DL` therefore uses a **fresh per-namespace sequence** (`001`, `002`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to the vault ADR it cross-references — mirroring `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001`. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet, matching the `B1-DL-018` / `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` precedent.)*
- **Subject.** Defect A — a suspected age-coupling in the skills-tracker ready-zone / honest-empty-state surface (`/app`, `app/index.html`). Diagnosed read-only against the shipped code; **not** the activities-modal list (that is Defect B / `B2-DL-002`).
- **Decision.** **No-fix. No divergence. No new ADR.** The read-only diagnosis confirms the ready-zone surface already behaves per vault **ADR-018** (the same readiness-as-pure-prerequisite-graph decision recorded for Б1 as `B1-DL-018`):
  1. **Ready-zone visibility is a pure prerequisite-graph property.** A skill's ready-zone membership is decided by `not-completed ∧ all-prerequisites-completed`; **age does not enter ready-zone visibility**. `isSkillReady` and `applyFilter` consume no age input for visibility.
  2. **Age is consumed only at the current-month highlight.** The single `currentLifeMonth` consumer on this surface is the tracker's current-month column highlight (a passive display reference), not any visibility/filter gate — consistent with ADR-018's "age demoted to passive display".
  3. **Honest-empty-state already holds.** When the ready-zone is empty the surface shows an honest message, not a silent empty table (the Б1 P3 honest-empty-state, `B1-DL-018` §4 / контракт §2) — so the condition Defect A suspected is already satisfied.
  - **Owner disposition.** Closed **no-fix**: the suspected defect is not present; the surface is correct as shipped. No code/copy change, no decision divergence, and **no new ADR** is warranted — the governing decision is the existing ADR-018.
- **Rationale.** Recording a read-only "no-fix, already-correct per ADR-018" disposition closes Defect A in the ledger without implying a silent change: a diagnosis that finds the code already honors the governing decision is itself an auditable outcome, and naming ADR-018 makes the basis explicit. Opening a new ADR for a non-divergent confirmation would inflate the decision surface for a finding that introduces no new decision.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Fold a defensive age-related guard into the ready-zone "just in case".** Rejected (owner) — there is nothing to fix; adding a guard would be behavior change against a correct surface and would re-introduce age into a visibility path ADR-018 deliberately kept age-free.
  2. **Open a new vault ADR for Defect A.** Rejected — no new decision is made; ADR-018 already governs. A no-fix confirmation maps to the existing ADR, the way `B1-DL-018` mapped to ADR-018.
  3. **Leave Defect A unrecorded (close silently).** Rejected — the milestone's P1 diagnosis is owed a ledger record so the no-fix disposition and its ADR-018 basis are auditable, even though it touched no files.
- **Supersedes.** None. Cross-references vault **ADR-018** (readiness as pure prerequisite graph; age demoted to display) and the repo-side `B1-DL-018` that first recorded it for track Б.
- **Effects.**
  - **No file change** from the P1 diagnosis (read-only; no edit, no commit) beyond this decision-log entry, added at Б2-CLOSE.
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — no enforced test / lint / schema / runtime guard is added (a no-fix confirmation enforces nothing new).
  - **No `app/` / schema / migration change.**
  - Pairs with `B2-DL-002` (Defect B — the activities-list relabel + de-age + impact-sort). Both are reconciled into `docs/execution-map.md` (the `Б2` Done block) at this close-out.

## B2-DL-002 — Б2 Defect B (Б-активности, = vault ADR-019): relabel + full de-age + impact-sort of the activities list; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-27
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen Б2 Defect-B ladder (P2 copy + P3 logic); no product-code / schema / migration change **this** packet (the functional changes shipped in P2 `689f30e` and P3 `d2652b4`). The activities list is frozen as shipped; this entry only records and reconciles.
- **Decision.** Record the **Б-активности** (activities-modal) correctness fix as shipped across `app/index.html` — the relabel + de-age copy (P2) and the de-age + impact-sort logic (P3). These are the orchestrator/owner-resolved **definitional refinements** of vault **ADR-019**'s impact-ordering for the activities surface — **not** a new architecture decision:
  1. **Relabel → "Подходящие активности"** (P2 `689f30e`). The section heading + coupled copy were de-aged off the prior age-worded framing.
  2. **Full de-age of the list (P3 `d2652b4`).** The `skill.age_start_months <= currentLifeMonth` filter is dropped, so the section shows **all unmastered skills** regardless of the child's age — the activities analogue of Б1's tracker de-age (`B1-DL-018`: no age gate). `currentLifeMonth` is no longer consumed by the activities filter/sort; its only remaining consumer is the unrelated tracker current-month highlight.
  3. **Impact-sort = DESCENDING count of DIRECT unmastered dependents (P3).** Ordering is by impact, where a skill's impact = the number of its **direct dependents** (skills listing it in `prerequisites[]`) that are **not** completed. The count is computed **front-side, read-only**, from a **reverse-prerequisite map** derived per modal-open from the existing dataset (`skill.id → skills listing it as a prereq`); **zero `/api`, zero dataset mutation**. This refines ADR-019's "impact-ordering" to a concrete, presentational metric.
  4. **Stable tie-break = dataset order (P3).** Equal-impact skills retain their original `milestonesData` order (native stable sort over a dataset-ordered array) — introducing **no** new normative ranking.
  5. **Presentational boundary (hard).** The impact-sort **reorders / populates an already-shown list**; it is **not** a "what to master next" gated recommendation (the Ступень-3 prescription Б1 refused). No UI label implies prescription; this is ordering only.
  6. **Zero-case copy reworded to the skills condition (P3).** After full de-age the list is empty **only when every unmastered skill is exhausted** (everything mastered). The age-worded message ("…соответствующих текущему возрасту ребёнка") is replaced by `Все навыки освоены — подходящих активностей сейчас нет` (`app/index.html:2554`).
  - **Copy lesson (low-ceremony, recorded inline).** Empty-state copy must word the **source-entity** condition (the skills are mastered), **not** attribute that state to the **derived items** (the activities). The activities are *suggested for* unmastered skills; they are not themselves "освоены". Two earlier candidate strings ("неосвоенных активностей не осталось" / a "Поздравляем …" variant) committed this category error and were rejected by the owner in favor of the shipped string above.
- **Rationale.** Full de-age makes the activities surface a complete unmastered-skill view (parity with the Б1 tracker de-age) instead of an age-clipped slice. Defining impact as the descending count of direct unmastered dependents, computed from a derived read-only reverse-prerequisite map, gives a concrete presentational ordering with no backend, no dataset mutation, and no new normative ranking (stable tie-break preserves dataset order). Holding the change strictly to reorder/populate keeps it inside the presentational boundary Б1 set — surfacing foundational skills first without becoming a prescription. Recording these as refinements of ADR-019 (not a new ADR) matches that the owner resolved them as definitional, not architectural.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Backend/enrichment computation of the dependents graph.** Rejected — front-only milestone; the reverse-prerequisite map is derivable read-only from the existing dataset at compute time, so `/api` involvement would be mis-scoped.
  2. **Age-floored list (status quo) or a secondary age sort.** Rejected — full de-age is the decided behavior (parity with `B1-DL-018`); a residual age input would re-clip the list the milestone deliberately opened.
  3. **A new UI label implying "master next" / a tunable impact knob.** Rejected — crosses the presentational boundary (prescription) and would convert a fixed presentational rule into a quality knob; the impact metric is **not** a config surface knob.
  4. **Ship one of the earlier zero-case strings ("неосвоенных активностей не осталось" / "Поздравляем …").** Rejected (owner) — category error: activities are derived suggestions, not masterable items; the empty-state must word the skills-mastered condition.
- **Supersedes.** None — first Defect-B entry. Cross-references vault **ADR-019** (the upstream impact-ordering decision for the activities surface; this entry is the repo-side definition site, mirroring how `B1-DL-018` cross-references ADR-018). Builds on `B1-DL-018` (track-Б de-age + presentational boundary + honest-empty-state precedent). *(Cross-ref note: this record **shares vault ADR-019 with `PWA-DL-001` by design** — ADR-019 is one sequencing decision that sanctioned both parts of the post-Б1 direction: PWA update-delivery (recorded by `PWA-DL-001`) and Б-активности impact-ordering (recorded here). One vault ADR feeding two repo milestones is the normal many-repo-to-one-vault pattern (cf. ADR-005 governing M1–M4); it is **not** a numbering collision and no reconciliation is owed.)*
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** read-only Defect-A diagnosis (no edit, no commit; `B2-DL-001`), **P2** `689f30e` (relabel + de-age activities copy → "Подходящие активности"), **P3** `d2652b4` (full de-age + impact-sort via the read-only reverse-prerequisite map; stable tie-break; zero-case copy reworded).
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `Б2 (ZPD activities-list correctness) — Done` sibling block added (P1–P3 + this Б2-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 / PWA Update-Delivery / L1 blocks and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - **Operability: none** — the change is a presentational reorder/de-age of an already-shown list. The impact metric is a **fixed presentational rule, not a tunable quality knob**, so it lands on **no** config surface and carries no `changed_in`; **no new signal** (the existing `activities_open` analytics event is unchanged). (The typed config/signal surfaces live in `/api` `parameters.py` / `signals.py`, untouched this front-only milestone.)
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — the milestone added no enforced test / lint / schema / runtime guard (`/app` is unguarded; `M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it; precedent: `B1-DL-018` / `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; a presentational reorder is not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `app/` change** this packet — the relabel/de-age/impact-sort + zero-case copy are frozen as shipped (P2 `689f30e`, P3 `d2652b4`); the dataset / `milestonesData` is byte-for-byte untouched.
