## BW-DL-001 — Biological-windows reference layer (front Layer-1): window→skill binding + ready-scoped urgency ranking + window highlight; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-29
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen bio-windows functional ladder (P2 `253c7f1` + the same-branch UX-debt `2d58b85`); the only product-behavior change recorded by this packet is the `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` bump (`v2`→`v3`), which delivers the already-shipped app-shell via the existing prompt-driven update banner and is the first app-shell change since the SW engine. The bio-windows binding / ranking / highlight / readiness logic is frozen as shipped; this entry records and reconciles. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `BW-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **biological-windows reference layer** (front Layer-1, PDR-004 band-first) — it is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), **not** the deploy-safety track (`L1-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. It is a fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to the vault ADRs it cross-references. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `B2-DL`.)*
- **Decision.** Record the biological-windows reference layer as shipped across `app/index.html` (P2 `253c7f1`) plus the same-branch UX-debt cleanup (`2d58b85`). These are the owner-resolved repo-side definition site for vault **ADR-021** / **ADR-022** (with **ADR-018** restored to its pure-prerequisite form):
  a. **Window→skill binding = window-side `applies_to:[skill_ids]`** in the embedded `bioWindowsData` const (`app/index.html`); `milestonesData` is byte-for-byte untouched (ADR-022 §1). The skill dataset carries no window back-reference — the binding lives entirely on the window side.
  b. **Predicate gate WITHDRAWN.** ADR-022 §2 (a window-driven predicate that could remove a skill from a surface) is **retracted by the 2026-06-28 owner amendment**; **ADR-018 is restored to a pure prerequisite graph**. Windows are a *reference/urgency* layer only — they **never remove a skill from any surface**. The withdrawal is the *absence* of a gate, not a new mechanism.
  c. **Activities urgency ranking metric (ADR-022 §3).** Within the activities modal, **Tier-1** skills that fall inside a window are ranked by `peak_fraction × criticality_weight(type, decline_shape)`; ★-marked skills are excluded from the windowed tier; sensitive windows are capped; attachment-type windows are front-loaded. **Tier-2** is the prior graph-impact order verbatim (descending count of direct unmastered dependents, stable dataset-order tie-break — the `B2-DL-002` metric).
  d. **Activities base set scoped to the ready (ZPD) subset (ADR-022 §3 amendment).** The activities base is narrowed to the **ready** subset via `isSkillReady` — the *same* prerequisite-readiness predicate as the readiness filter — before the Tier-1/Tier-2 ranking is applied.
  e. **Window highlight in the activities modal only (§4).** Critical windows highlight `#FBBF24`, sensitive windows `#FEF3C7` — reusing the existing amber palette. The graph/tracker table is untouched.
  f. **Secondary (same branch, `2d58b85`) — UX-debt.** The redundant completed-skills filter (`#filterToggleBtn` + its machinery) was removed and the readiness filter renamed; onboarding step count 3→2. Recorded here as a secondary effect of the same milestone, not a separate decision.
- **Rationale.** Window-side `applies_to` keeps `milestonesData` immutable and the binding auditable in one place. The predicate-gate withdrawal follows the owner's 2026-06-28 reversal: windows inform urgency but must not hide skills, so ADR-018 returns to a pure prerequisite graph and the layer stays strictly additive/presentational. Defining urgency as `peak_fraction × criticality_weight` over the windowed Tier-1, with Tier-2 falling back to the existing graph-impact order, surfaces time-sensitive skills first without a backend or a new normative ranking. Scoping the base to the ready subset via the existing `isSkillReady` keeps the activities surface consistent with the readiness filter (one predicate, not two). Reusing the existing amber palette avoids introducing a new hue for a reference cue. The UX-debt cleanup removes a filter made redundant by the readiness rename.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Keep the ADR-022 §2 predicate gate (windows remove skills).** Rejected (owner, 2026-06-28 amendment) — a window must not hide a skill; ADR-018 restored to a pure prerequisite graph and the layer kept reference-only.
  2. **Mirror the binding onto `milestonesData` (skill-side window ids).** Rejected — opens the frozen dataset; window-side `applies_to` carries the binding with the dataset untouched.
  3. **Rank the full uncompleted set (the `B2-DL-002` all-unmastered base).** Rejected — the activities base is now the ready (prerequisite) subset; ranking an age- or readiness-unfiltered set would re-widen the surface the readiness predicate scopes.
  4. **A new highlight hue for windows.** Rejected — reuse the existing amber palette (`#FBBF24` / `#FEF3C7`); a new hue adds a color contract for a reference cue.
  5. **Split the UX-debt into a separate `BW-DL-002`.** Rejected — single close-out (L1 / PWA precedent); the cleanup has no independent decision surface and is recorded as a secondary effect.
- **Supersedes.** Supersedes the **"activities = all uncompleted skills"** part of `B2-DL-002` (Decision §2: "the section shows **all unmastered skills**"). The activities base is now the **ready (prerequisite) subset** via `isSkillReady` — the narrowing is by the **prerequisite-readiness graph, NOT by age** (no age filter returns; ADR-018 stays intact and is in fact restored to pure-prerequisite form by §b above). The `B2-DL-002` impact-ordering (now Tier-2) is retained verbatim as the within-tier order. Cross-references vault **ADR-021** / **ADR-022** (the upstream window-layer decisions; this entry is the repo-side definition site) and **ADR-018** (pure prerequisite graph, restored).
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P2** `253c7f1` (`bioWindowsData` + ready-scoped activities urgency ranking + window highlight); **UX-debt** `2d58b85` (drop redundant completed-skills filter + rename readiness filter; onboarding 3→2).
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `Bio-windows reference layer — Done` sibling block added (P2 + UX-debt + this BW-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 / PWA / L1 / Б2 blocks and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` bumped `v2`→`v3` this packet (ships the updated app shell via the existing prompt-driven banner). **In the same edit** the owed `app/sw.js` `changed_in` placeholder (`<Б-DL-NNN, assigned in P3>`) is **physically backfilled to `PWA-DL-001`** — discharging the deferral recorded in `PWA-DL-001` §5 (deferred precisely to the next legitimate `CACHE_VERSION` bump to avoid a spurious no-op update prompt). The backfilled id is `PWA-DL-001`'s, not `BW-DL`'s.
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — front-only presentational milestone; the predicate-gate withdrawal is the *absence* of a gate and nothing is newly machine-enforced (`/app` unguarded; `M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it; precedent: `B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL-002` / `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; a reference/urgency layer is not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `milestonesData` / schema / migration change** — the dataset is byte-for-byte untouched; `bioWindowsData` / ranking / highlight / readiness logic frozen as shipped. No `app/index.html` change this packet (close-out is docs + sw.js only).

## ICON-DL-001 — PWA Logo & Favicon: brand icon/favicon set across manifest + head + SW precache; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-29
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen P1–P3 functional ladder (`d86b14f` / `a133345` / `eae43d1`); no product-code / schema / migration change this packet. The brand-icon asset set and its wiring are frozen as shipped; this entry only records and reconciles. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `ICON-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **PWA logo & favicon** track — it is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`, the update-delivery milestone — a separate namespace this packet must not borrow), **not** the deploy-safety track (`L1-DL-001`), **not** the bio-windows track (`BW-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. It is a fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to the vault ADR it cross-references. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `BW-DL-001` / `B2-DL`.)*
- **Decision.** Record the PWA logo & favicon milestone as shipped across `app/manifest.json` (P1), `app/index.html` head (P2), and `app/sw.js` (P3):
  1. **Brand icons in the manifest (P1 `d86b14f`).** The manifest `icons` array carries the brand logo at both `purpose: "any"` sizes (`/icons/icon-logo-192.png`, `/icons/icon-logo-512.png`) and both `purpose: "maskable"` sizes (`/icons/maskable-192.png`, `/icons/maskable-512.png`) — the install / home-screen / splash surface.
  2. **Favicon + apple-touch head links (P2 `a133345`).** `app/index.html` `<head>` declares `rel="icon"` PNGs at 16×16 / 32×32 (`/icons/favicon-16.png` / `favicon-32.png`) and `rel="apple-touch-icon"` at 180×180 (`/icons/apple-touch-icon.png`) — the browser-tab + iOS add-to-home surface.
  3. **SW precache + `CACHE_VERSION` bump (P3 `eae43d1`).** `app/sw.js` `OFFLINE_URLS` precaches the install-surface set — the four manifest icons + `apple-touch-icon` — plus `favicon-16/32`, and `CACHE_VERSION` is bumped `v3`→`v4` so the existing network-first SW + `#updateBanner` path (`PWA-DL-001`) delivers the icons to already-installed users; `activate()` purges the `theygrow-v3` cache on the bump. The two dead placeholder paths (`/icons/icon-192.png`, `/icons/icon-512.png`) are dropped from `OFFLINE_URLS` (no longer manifest-referenced after P1; precaching dead assets wastes cache).
  4. **New-filename asset scheme (versioning-by-filename).** The brand assets ship under **new filenames** (`icon-logo-`, `maskable-`, `apple-touch-icon`, `favicon-*`) rather than overwriting the old placeholders (`icon-192/512.png`). Because the URLs are new, a `CACHE_VERSION` bump's `addAll` fetches them **fresh** — there is no immutable-cached stale-asset hazard from reusing a filename. This is the repo-side application of vault **ADR-024** (static-asset versioning-by-filename invariant): change the bytes ⇒ change the URL.
  5. **Gate #5 narrowed `both` → PNG-only (no `favicon.ico`).** The favicon surface is delivered **PNG-only** via `rel="icon"` (16/32) — a discrete `favicon.ico` was **not** landed. The bare `/favicon.ico` browser probe is left **as-is** (it 404s harmlessly; the PNG `rel="icon"` links cover the tab). The earlier "both (`.ico` + PNG)" reading of gate #5 is narrowed here to PNG-only as the shipped scope.
- **Rationale.** Splitting the surface three ways — manifest (install), head (tab + iOS), SW (offline/installed delivery) — lands each icon role where the platform reads it, and routing installed-user delivery through the existing `CACHE_VERSION` + `#updateBanner` path reuses the `PWA-DL-001` engine rather than inventing a second delivery mechanism. The new-filename scheme (ADR-024) is the decisive choice: overwriting `icon-192/512.png` in place would risk an immutable-cached client serving the old bytes under the old URL, whereas a new URL is fetched fresh on the bump — so dropping the now-dead placeholders from the precache costs nothing and reclaims cache. PNG-only keeps the favicon surface to the formats every current target honours without authoring a multi-resolution `.ico`; the `/favicon.ico` probe 404 is harmless given the explicit PNG `rel="icon"` links.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Overwrite the placeholder filenames in place (`icon-192/512.png`).** Rejected — reusing a URL for changed bytes is the immutable-cache stale-asset hazard ADR-024 exists to prevent; new filenames are fetched fresh on the `CACHE_VERSION` bump.
  2. **Land a discrete `favicon.ico` too (the "both" reading of gate #5).** Rejected (owner) — PNG `rel="icon"` covers the tab on every current target; a multi-res `.ico` adds an asset and a build step for no reach this milestone needs. Gate #5 narrowed to PNG-only.
  3. **Add a `/favicon.ico` redirect / route so the bare probe resolves.** Rejected — the probe 404 is harmless with explicit PNG `rel="icon"` links; a redirect adds nginx surface for a cosmetic non-issue.
  4. **Skip the `CACHE_VERSION` bump (rely on cache-first re-fetch).** Rejected — installed users would not pick up the new manifest-referenced icons without the bump; the bump + `#updateBanner` is the `PWA-DL-001` delivery path.
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `ICON-DL` namespace. Cross-references vault **ADR-024** (static-asset versioning-by-filename invariant; this entry is the repo-side definition site, mirroring how `PWA-DL-001` cross-references vault ADR-019). Builds on `PWA-DL-001` (the network-first SW + `#updateBanner` delivery engine this milestone rides), the M2-P1 monorepo split (PWA under `/app`), and the `app/icons/` asset surface.
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** `d86b14f` (manifest `any` + `maskable` brand icons), **P2** `a133345` (favicon + apple-touch-icon head links), **P3** `eae43d1` (SW precache of the install-surface set + `favicon-16/32`; `CACHE_VERSION` `v3`→`v4`; placeholder paths dropped).
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `PWA Logo & Favicon — Done` sibling block added (P1/P2/P3 + this PWA-ICON-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 / PWA Update-Delivery / L1 / Б2 / Bio-windows blocks and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine (cross-cutting front-only track — not naively "next" in that ladder).
  - `docs/RUNBOOK.md` "What runs today" enumerates the shipped install-surface icon set (manifest `any` 192/512 + `maskable` 192/512, head `favicon-16/32` + `apple-touch-icon` 180, precached in `sw.js v4`) and records the `favicon.ico`-absent / PNG-only disposition.
  - **No `app/` change** this packet — manifest (P1), head (P2), and `sw.js v4` (P3) are frozen as shipped; close-out is docs only.
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — front-only asset milestone; nothing newly machine-enforced (`/app` unguarded; `M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it; precedent: `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `BW-DL-001` added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; a brand-icon asset set is not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `milestonesData` / schema / migration change** — no dataset or backend surface touched.

## GWN-DL-001 — graph-why-now (front Layer-1): downstream "Открывает дальше" + readiness "Почему сейчас" / ✓○ prereq markers + graph-nav GA; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-29
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen graph-why-now functional ladder (P1 `7f0289c` + P2 `01e8949`). The only product-behavior changes this packet are the `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` bump (`v4`→`v5`) and **one** GA `graph_navigate` event added to the existing delegated chip handler in `app/index.html`; the P1/P2 graph-explanation logic is frozen as shipped and this entry records and reconciles. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `GWN-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **graph-why-now** track (front Layer-1, band-first) — it is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), **not** the deploy-safety track (`L1-DL-001`), **not** the bio-windows track (`BW-DL-001`), **not** the PWA logo/favicon track (`ICON-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. It is a fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to the vault ADR it cross-references. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `BW-DL-001` / `ICON-DL-001` / `B2-DL`.)*
- **Decision.** Record the graph-why-now explanatory layer as shipped across `app/index.html` (P1 `7f0289c`, P2 `01e8949`):
  a. **Downstream "Открывает дальше" (P1 `7f0289c`).** A new modal section (`getDownstreamHtml`) renders the skills a given skill unlocks — a reverse scan over `DATA.milestones` for skills whose `prerequisites` include the current id, as clickable chips. A graph leaf (no dependents) renders **honest empty** through the absence of the section (mirrors `getPrerequisitesHtml`). Edge source = the `prerequisites` fields (the same graph as `impactById` / `isSkillReady`); `milestonesData` is byte-for-byte untouched.
  b. **Readiness "Почему сейчас" + ✓/○ prerequisite markers (P2 `01e8949`).** A new modal block (`getReadinessHtml`) states readiness via **`isSkillReady`** — the single readiness authority, the **ADR-018 pure-prerequisite predicate** (not-completed ∧ all prerequisites completed; no age, no biological windows). Language is population-level and non-normative; the three states are mastered / ready / blocked, and the blocked state lists **only the un-mastered direct prerequisites** as clickable chips. The "Требуемые навыки" prerequisites list (`getPrerequisitesHtml`) gains ✓/○ per-prerequisite completion markers.
  c. **`CACHE_VERSION` `v4`→`v5` (this packet).** Bumped in `app/sw.js` so the already-shipped P1/P2 sections — inline in `index.html`, served **network-first** (`sw.js` navigation strategy) — reach installed PWAs via the existing prompt-driven `#updateBanner` update channel (`PWA-DL-001`, vault ADR-019); `activate()` purges `theygrow-v4` on the bump. **No `OFFLINE_URLS` change** — finding recorded: the feature adds **no new asset or route** (all changes are inline in `index.html`), so the precache set is unchanged. The `changed_in: PWA-DL-001` comment was already physically backfilled at BW-CLOSE — no owed placeholder remained.
  d. **Graph-navigation GA event (this packet).** A single `trackEvent('graph_navigate', { from_skill, to_skill, relation })` fires at the **existing** delegated chip handler on `#skillModal`, before `openSkillModal` rewrites the modal body. `relation ∈ {downstream, prerequisite, readiness}` is carried by a `data-relation` attribute added to the chip spans in the three renderers (enabling metadata on existing spans — **not** additional emitters). `from_skill` / `to_skill` are **catalog skill names** (same privacy class as `skill_name` in the existing `skill_view` event) — no family/child data. The destination view was already captured by `skill_view` (fires on every modal open); `graph_navigate` adds the **traversal interaction** and which relation drove it.
- **Rationale.** Surfacing downstream dependents and a plain-language "why now" turns the tracker from a flat list into an explorable graph with explanations — the explanations-and-trust intent of the band-first roadmap — without a backend, a dataset change, or a new normative ranking. Anchoring readiness on the existing `isSkillReady` keeps **one** readiness authority across the row markers, the filter, and this block (not a second predicate). Routing installed-user delivery through the `CACHE_VERSION` + `#updateBanner` path reuses the `PWA-DL-001` engine rather than inventing a second mechanism; leaving `OFFLINE_URLS` untouched is correct because nothing new is fetched. A single `graph_navigate` emitter at the one delegated handler, classified by a `data-relation` attribute, measures graph traversal at one instrumentation point instead of three per-section emitters.
- **No dedicated ADR by design.** graph-why-now has **no** dedicated vault ADR. The feature traces to **roadmap §1 п.3** / **PDR-004 §3** (band-first) and the explanations-and-trust product vision; it **cross-references vault [[adr-018]]** only for the readiness predicate (`isSkillReady`) it reuses. Recording the absence of a dedicated ADR is deliberate — the layer is additive/presentational over the existing prerequisite graph and introduces no new architectural contract.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **A distinct readiness predicate for the "Почему сейчас" block.** Rejected — `isSkillReady` (ADR-018) is the single readiness authority; a second predicate would risk drift against the row markers and the filter.
  2. **Per-section GA emitters (one `trackEvent` in each renderer / three handlers).** Rejected — the one delegated handler already catches every chip; a `data-relation` attribute classifies the section at a single emitter (no fabricated emitters elsewhere).
  3. **Rely on `skill_view` alone for graph navigation.** Rejected — `skill_view` captures the destination view but not the traversal interaction or its source relation; `graph_navigate` is the minimal addition that does.
  4. **Skip the `CACHE_VERSION` bump.** Rejected — installed users would not pick up the inline P1/P2 sections without the bump + `#updateBanner` (the `PWA-DL-001` delivery path).
  5. **Treat the GA event as a `theygrow_api.signals` / `parameters` surface.** Rejected — it is `/app` frontend product analytics via the existing `trackEvent`→gtag path, not the `/api` operability surface (see Effects).
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `GWN-DL` namespace. Cross-references vault **[[adr-018]]** (the pure-prerequisite readiness predicate this layer reuses; the repo-side definition site is `B1-DL-018`). Builds on `PWA-DL-001` (the network-first SW + `#updateBanner` delivery engine the cache bump rides) and the existing `app/index.html` skill-modal / `trackEvent` surfaces.
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** `7f0289c` (downstream "Открывает дальше" section), **P2** `01e8949` (readiness "Почему сейчас" block + ✓/○ prerequisite completion markers).
  - This packet's `app/` changes: `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` `v4`→`v5` (one line; `OFFLINE_URLS` untouched); `app/index.html` gains three `data-relation` attributes on the existing chip spans + one `trackEvent('graph_navigate', …)` at the existing delegated handler. No other behavior change; `milestonesData` byte-for-byte untouched.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `graph-why-now — Done` sibling block added (P1 + P2 + this GWN-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 / PWA / L1 / Б2 / Bio-windows / PWA-Icon blocks and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - **Operability: none** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). The `graph_navigate` event is `/app` product analytics via the existing `trackEvent` path — **not** a `theygrow_api.parameters` config knob and **not** a `theygrow_api.signals` SignalSink taxonomy signal.
  - **No `docs/RUNBOOK.md` entry** — "what runs today" is unchanged (no new route/asset/infra; the cache bump rides the existing SW + update-banner path already documented).
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — front-only presentational milestone; nothing newly machine-enforced (`/app` unguarded; `M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it; precedent: `B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL-002` / `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `BW-DL-001` / `ICON-DL-001` added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; a graph-explanation layer is not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `milestonesData` / schema / migration change** — the dataset is byte-for-byte untouched; the P1/P2 graph-explanation logic is frozen as shipped.

## SLA-DL-001 — skill-link-from-activities (front Layer-1): activity-card titles deep-link to the skill detail modal; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-06-30
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen skill-link-from-activities functional ladder (P1 `cc768c7`). The only product-behavior change this packet is the `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` bump (`v5`→`v6`); the P1 deep-link wiring is frozen as shipped and this entry records and reconciles, after a behavioural smoke confirmed the shipped flow (see Effects). *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `SLA-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **skill-link-from-activities** track (front Layer-1, band-first) — it is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), **not** the deploy-safety track (`L1-DL-001`), **not** the bio-windows track (`BW-DL-001`), **not** the PWA logo/favicon track (`ICON-DL-001`), **not** the graph-why-now track (`GWN-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. It is a fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to any vault ADR. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `BW-DL-001` / `ICON-DL-001` / `GWN-DL-001` / `B2-DL`.)*
- **Decision.** Record the activity-card → skill-modal deep link as shipped in `app/index.html` (P1 `cc768c7`):
  a. **Activity-card titles become keyboard-activatable buttons (P1 `cc768c7`).** Each `.activity-card-title` in the activities modal grid (`openActivitiesModal`) carries `data-skill-id`, `role="button"`, `tabindex="0"`, and an `aria-label` ("Открыть навык: <name>"). A single delegated handler on `#activitiesGrid` resolves **click** and **keydown** (`Enter` / `Space`, with `preventDefault()` so `Space` does not scroll) through one shared activation path (`activateActivitySkill`), which opens the **existing** `openSkillModal(skill)` — no change to its signature or body. The skill modal stacks **over** the still-open activities modal (z-index **120 > 100**) and the **first** back/close returns to the activities modal (the activity entry point pushes **no** skill-history); prerequisite/downstream chip navigation continues to walk skill↔skill via the existing `#skillModal` handler (which **does** push history). `milestonesData` byte-for-byte untouched.
  b. **`activity_skill_open` GA event (P1 `cc768c7`).** A single `trackEvent('activity_skill_open', { skill_name })` fires in `activateActivitySkill` **before** `openSkillModal`, mirroring the `graph_navigate` ordering so the source is captured before the modal body is rewritten. `skill_name` is a catalog skill name (same privacy class as `skill_view`'s `skill_name`) — no family/child data.
  c. **`CACHE_VERSION` `v5`→`v6` (this packet).** Bumped in `app/sw.js` so the already-shipped P1 inline change in `index.html`, served **network-first**, reaches installed PWAs via the existing prompt-driven `#updateBanner` update channel (`PWA-DL-001`, vault ADR-019); `activate()` purges `theygrow-v5` on the bump. **No `OFFLINE_URLS` change** — the feature adds **no** new asset or route (all changes are inline in `index.html`), so the precache set is unchanged. The `changed_in: PWA-DL-001` rationale comment stays as-is (the delivery knob's defining decision is not re-pinned per packet).
- **Rationale.** Deep-linking activity-card titles into the existing skill detail modal turns the "what to do this month" activities surface into a jumping-off point for the full skill explanation — the explanations-and-trust intent of the band-first roadmap — by **reusing** `openSkillModal` and the existing `trackEvent` path, with no backend, no dataset change, and no new modal. One delegated handler with a shared click/keyboard activation path keeps mouse and keyboard activation from diverging and makes the titles accessible (`role="button"` / `tabindex` / `aria-label`). Not pushing skill-history on the activity entry point keeps the first back/close returning to where the user came from (the activities modal), while chip navigation retains its skill↔skill walk. Routing installed-user delivery through the `CACHE_VERSION` + `#updateBanner` path reuses the `PWA-DL-001` engine rather than inventing a second mechanism; leaving `OFFLINE_URLS` untouched is correct because nothing new is fetched.
- **No dedicated ADR by design.** skill-link-from-activities has **no** dedicated vault ADR. The feature traces to **roadmap §1 п.4** / **PDR-004 §3** (band-first) and the explanations-and-trust product vision; it is additive/presentational over the existing skill modal and introduces no new architectural contract. **Divergence from roadmap §1 п.4 stated explicitly:** this milestone closes the **deep-link predecessor only** — activity titles now reach the skill detail — and does **NOT** deliver the full roadmap §1 п.4 browseable/searchable 174-skill reference, which stays ⬜ open/gated. Recording the absence of a dedicated ADR is deliberate, mirroring `GWN-DL-001` / `ICON-DL-001`.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Per-card / per-handler GA emitters.** Rejected — one delegated handler on `#activitiesGrid` already catches every card title; a single `activity_skill_open` emitter in the shared activation path avoids fabricated emitters and matches the `graph_navigate` precedent.
  2. **Separate click and keyboard activation paths.** Rejected — click and keydown resolving the same `.activity-card-title[data-skill-id]` through one `activateActivitySkill` guarantees mouse and keyboard cannot diverge.
  3. **Push the activity entry onto the skill-history stack.** Rejected — the first back/close should return to the activities modal the user came from; pushing history would make back re-open the same skill. Chip navigation still pushes (skill↔skill walk).
  4. **Rely on `skill_view` alone.** Rejected — `skill_view` captures the destination view but not that the user arrived from an activity card; `activity_skill_open` is the minimal addition that records the entry point.
  5. **Skip the `CACHE_VERSION` bump.** Rejected — installed users would not pick up the inline P1 change without the bump + `#updateBanner` (the `PWA-DL-001` delivery path).
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `SLA-DL` namespace. Builds on `PWA-DL-001` (the network-first SW + `#updateBanner` delivery engine the cache bump rides) and the existing `app/index.html` skill-modal (`openSkillModal`) / `trackEvent` surfaces.
- **Effects.**
  - Functional ladder (frozen, shipped before this packet): **P1** `cc768c7` (activity-card titles → `openSkillModal` deep link via a delegated click + Enter/Space handler; `activity_skill_open` GA event).
  - **Behaviourally verified this packet** (headless gstack smoke, app served over http): click / Enter / Space on a card title opens the skill modal stacked over the still-open activities modal (z-index 120 > 100); the first back/close returns to the activities modal (no skill-history push from the activity entry point); prerequisite/downstream chip navigation walks skill↔skill (history push, back pops skill→skill then closes to activities); `activity_skill_open` (with `skill_name`) and `graph_navigate` events fire; no console errors.
  - This packet's `app/` change: `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` `v5`→`v6` (one line; `OFFLINE_URLS` and the rationale comment untouched). No `app/index.html` change; `milestonesData` byte-for-byte untouched.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `skill-link-from-activities — Done` sibling block added (P1 + this SLA-CLOSE), placed alongside the Б1 / PWA Update-Delivery / L1 / Б2 / Bio-windows / PWA Logo & Favicon / graph-why-now blocks and **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - `docs/RUNBOOK.md` reconciled this packet: the stale `CACHE_VERSION v4` reference (left stale by `GWN-CLOSE`, which bumped code to `v5`) is corrected to `v6` to match the shipped service worker.
  - **Operability: none** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). The `activity_skill_open` event is `/app` product analytics via the existing `trackEvent` path — **not** a `theygrow_api.parameters` config knob and **not** a `theygrow_api.signals` SignalSink taxonomy signal.
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — front-only presentational milestone; nothing newly machine-enforced (`/app` unguarded; `M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it; precedent: `B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL-002` / `PWA-DL-001` / `L1-DL-001` / `BW-DL-001` / `ICON-DL-001` / `GWN-DL-001` added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; an activity→skill deep link is not a §2/§4 product invariant.
  - **No `milestonesData` / schema / migration change** — the dataset is byte-for-byte untouched; the P1 deep-link logic is frozen as shipped.

## VDK-DL-001 — vendor-domain-kb-artifact (M(В)-1): app becomes a pure reader of the vendored KB artifact kb-v1.json; close-out

- **Date.** 2026-07-04
- **Type.** Decision record + docs reconciliation — close-out of the frozen vendor-domain-kb-artifact ladder (P2 `d8c60f6`, P3 `82b9685`; P1 was a read-only diagnosis/design packet — no commit). **No product-behavior change this packet** (docs-only): unlike `SLA-CLOSE`, the `CACHE_VERSION` bump (`v6`→`v7`) already landed inside P3 as part of the swap's delivery surface. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `VDK-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the **M(В)-1 vendor-domain-kb-artifact** track — the data/KB ladder whose curation home is the separate `theygrow-domain-kb` repo. It is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), **not** the deploy-safety track (`L1-DL-001`), **not** the bio-windows track (`BW-DL-001`), **not** the icon track (`ICON-DL-001`), **not** the front Layer-1 tracks (`GWN-DL-001` / `SLA-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. Fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), matching the `VDK-P{k}` packet markers already carried in code. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring every prior non-spine namespace.)*
- **Decision.** End dual-home: the KB's only home is `theygrow-domain-kb`; the app consumes its published artifact and nothing else. Recorded as delivered:
  a. **Tag-anchored sync — the vendoring mechanism (P2 `d8c60f6`).** `scripts/sync-kb-artifact.sh <N>` vendors the published `compiled/kb-v{N}.json` into `app/kb-v{N}.json` byte-identically, anchored to the domain-kb **release tag** `kb-v{N}` (vault ADR-026 §2 tag-anchor annotation), never floating `main` — `compiled/` holds only the current version, so the tag is the only reproducible re-sync anchor for a frozen version. Owner-run, RUNBOOK-style, deliberately **not** wired into pre-commit/CI; toolchain-free (git + coreutils). `.pre-commit-config.yaml` excludes `app/kb-v1\.json` from the rewriting hooks (byte-as-published; `check-json` still validates it read-only).
  b. **Async fetch-then-init boot — the swap (P3 `82b9685`).** The inline `milestonesData` script block and the `bioWindowsData` literal (including its provenance block comment — that narrative now lives structurally in domain-kb) are deleted from `app/index.html` (−607 KB). Bindings (`DATA`, `windowsBySkillId`, `MIN_MONTH`, `MAX_MONTH`, `CATEGORIES`) hoisted to module-scope `let`; `kbReady = fetch('/kb-v1.json')` starts at eval time; `initData(kb)` wires `DATA = adaptNewDataFormat(kb.milestones)` and the windows-index from `kb.bio_windows` (adapter and builder **bodies unchanged** — swap of source, not logic); `DOMContentLoaded` gates on `kbReady.then(initData → init()).catch(showKbLoadError)`; `showKbLoadError` renders a minimal honest error + reload affordance (honest degradation — no silent blank app).
  c. **Static delivery of the artifact (P3 `82b9685`).** `app/sw.js`: `CACHE_VERSION` `v6`→`v7` + `/kb-v1.json` added to `OFFLINE_URLS` (the existing Cache-First static strategy is already correct for an immutable artifact — fetch handler untouched); installed users migrate via the `PWA-DL-001` path. `app/nginx.conf`: a **narrow** immutable location `^/kb-v[0-9]+\.json$` (`Cache-Control: public, immutable, max-age=31536000`) per the vault ADR-024 narrow-pattern annotation (owner gate 2026-07-04) — the generic static regex stays `.json`-free and the `manifest.json` block is untouched. `app/Dockerfile`: `COPY kb-v1.json` into the nginx html root (build context already `./app`; no cloudbuild change).
  d. **Goal met.** Dual-home ended: the app is a pure READER of the vendored versioned artifact under the KB-artifact contract (in-memory shape via the adapter; additive-evolution tolerance; no code import from domain-kb; byte-as-published — the app never edits KB data). Enforced-side seam recorded as `VDK-P3-INV-001`.
- **Rationale.** A single curation home (domain-kb) with a published, versioned, byte-frozen artifact removes the drift risk that dual-home guaranteed. Versioning-by-filename (ADR-024) makes the artifact safely immutable-cacheable at every layer (nginx, SW precache, browser); the release-tag anchor (ADR-026 §2) makes any historical version reproducibly re-vendorable; a reader-only app keeps KB curation, schema evolution, and data-quality work out of the product repo.
- **Cross-references.** Vault **ADR-026** (domain-kb extraction; §2 tag-anchor annotation) — this entry is the **repo-side definition site** for ADR-026 (first decision-log reference), mirroring how `ICON-DL-001` cross-references vault ADR-024. Also vault **ADR-024** (static-asset versioning-by-filename; narrow-pattern annotation, owner gate 2026-07-04) via `ICON-DL-001`, and vault ADR-020 (promotion gate — the RUNBOOK smoke now checks the served artifact). Pointers only; the annotations are not restated here.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Sync from floating `main` instead of the release tag.** Rejected — `compiled/` holds only the current version (KB-artifact contract §1); once it bumps past N, `main` can no longer reproduce a frozen version. The tag is the only stable anchor.
  2. **Keep the inline data as a fallback behind the fetch.** Rejected — dual-home is the disease being cured; a fallback copy silently regrows drift and doubles the payload. Honest degradation (`showKbLoadError`) replaces it.
  3. **Broad `.json` extension in the nginx immutable regex.** Rejected — it would catch `manifest.json` (which has its own 1-hour block) and any future non-immutable JSON; the owner-gated ADR-024 annotation fixes the **narrow** `^/kb-v[0-9]+\.json$` pattern.
  4. **Runtime KB endpoint on `/api`.** Rejected/gated — server KB is out of this milestone's perimeter; a static immutable artifact rides the existing PWA delivery surface with zero new runtime.
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `VDK-DL` namespace. Builds on `ICON-DL-001` (repo-side ADR-024 site whose versioning-by-filename scheme the artifact reuses) and `PWA-DL-001` (the network-first SW + `#updateBanner` delivery engine the v7 bump rides).
- **Effects.**
  - Frozen ladder: **P1** read-only diagnosis + design (async-boot design, nginx fork-B, sync mechanics; no commit — design fixed in this entry), **P2** `d8c60f6`, **P3** `82b9685`.
  - **Validation evidence (P3, recorded):** deep-equal data parity pre-swap (`kb.milestones` ≡ inline `milestonesData` modulo the never-read top-level `edges` key; `kb.bio_windows` ≡ inline `bioWindowsData`, 12/12 deep-equal); DOM-parity **IDENTICAL** via per-section SHA-256 on isolated origins — table head/body, all 174 skill-modal bodies, activities top-note/grid, Tier-1/Tier-2 order + window classes, deep-link result — zero console errors; offline boot from the v7 precache with the server killed (app rebuilt fully, kb fetch resolved from cache); error path verified on an origin without the artifact; artifact sha256 `03a71f2e6336095d0e7fa8ffd575e32bceae8d557e5c8e721e28c40537dcc9ab` identical before/after (byte-as-published); `pre-commit run --all-files` green.
  - **Accepted deviations (P3 report, a–d):** (a) the provenance comment actually spanned `:1827–1838` (packet said `:1830–1838`) — deleted in full per intent; (b) the sixth binding (`bioWindowsData` successor) was **not** hoisted — `kb.bio_windows` is consumed locally inside `initData` (its only reader), per the "as applicable" latitude; (c) three comment-only updates where comments named the deleted `milestonesData` variable; (d) `nginx -t` not runnable locally (no Docker daemon) — the first container start validates the config.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `vendor-domain-kb-artifact (M(В)-1) — Done` sibling block added (P1–P3 + this VDK-CLOSE), **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - `docs/RUNBOOK.md` reconciled this packet: new **KB artifact (owner-run sync + version bump)** section (sync procedure + version-bump recipe); `app/kb-v1.json` added to the "what runs today" asset list; the stale `CACHE_VERSION v6` reference (left stale by VDK-P3, which bumped code to `v7`) corrected — the `SLA-DL-001` precedent; the promotion smoke gains the two kb checks (immutable `Cache-Control`; served sha256 == vendored, the ADR-020 gate carry from the P3 report).
  - `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry **`VDK-P3-INV-001`** added (the vendoring-seam invariant; first non-`M{N}` INV namespace, owner-decided 2026-07-04).
  - **Operability: none across the ladder** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). The `CACHE_VERSION` bump is mechanical operation of the existing `PWA-DL-001` knob; the `/kb-v1.json` fetch-URL literal is filename-versioning (a new artifact version is a new file + code change), not a runtime knob; the nginx `max-age=31536000` is a constant inside the immutable-pattern invariant, not a tunable.
  - **Carried follow-ups:** the KB-artifact-contract **consumer rule** (skill-id stability across `kb-v{N}` — canonical ids never renamed/dropped between versions; family `localStorage` keys + future paid-graph refs anchor on them) — drafted in the P3 report, **owner applies it in `theygrow-domain-kb` `docs/kb-artifact-contract.md`** (not this repo); the two frozen-174 data defects remain **curation carries** in domain-kb — they ride along byte-as-published, and their presence post-swap is correct.
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — that file's scope is `AGENTS.md` §2 / §4 product-invariant restatements; the vendoring seam is a build/delivery contract, not a §2/§4 product invariant.

## GA-DL-001 — ga4-instrumentation-depth (front analytics): cross-navigation instrumentation + telemetry PII fix

- **Date.** 2026-07-25
- **Type.** Decision record — single-packet front-only analytics milestone; the code lands in this same packet (`app/index.html` + this ledger reconcile), not a close-out of frozen commits. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `GA-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **GA4 instrumentation depth** track — it is **not** track-Б (`B1-DL-018` / `B2-DL`), **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), **not** the deploy-safety track (`L1-DL-001`), **not** the bio-windows track (`BW-DL-001`), **not** the icon track (`ICON-DL-001`), **not** the front Layer-1 tracks (`GWN-DL-001` / `SLA-DL-001`), **not** the data/KB track (`VDK-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. Fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to any vault ADR. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring every prior non-spine namespace.)*
- **Decision.** Deepen the existing `trackEvent`→gtag surface in `app/index.html` so cross-navigation behavior is analyzable, and remove the one live child-PII telemetry leak:
  a. **`skill_view` `source` param.** `openSkillModal` gains a third param `source` (default sentinel `'unknown'`, never expected), threaded from all four open paths: `table_row`, `${relation}_chip` (→ `prerequisite_chip` / `downstream_chip` / `readiness_chip`, derived from the existing `data-relation` attribute at the delegated chip handler), `activity_card`, `back_navigation` (the back branch of `navigateBackOrCloseSkillModal`). Back-hops thereby become visible as `skill_view` `source=back_navigation`; unique-view analyses should exclude that source.
  b. **`graph_navigate` depth + ids.** The chip-handler event gains `nav_depth: skillModalHistory.length + 1` — the 1-based ordinal of the hop in the chain (the event fires before the history push inside `openSkillModal`, so bare `length` would report 0 for the first hop; back-pops decrement first, so A→B→back→C correctly re-reports 1) — plus `from_skill_id` / `to_skill_id` alongside the existing name params.
  c. **`skill_modal_close` chain-end event.** One event fired exclusively inside `closeSkillModal` (the unique choke point both close paths funnel through), guarded on the modal actually being open: `action` (`close_button` — ✕ with empty history — or `backdrop`), `nav_depth: skillModalHistory.length` (chain depth abandoned; deliberately bare `length` — 0 = closed without ever navigating), `skill_id` of the skill on screen. **No dedicated "back" event** — it would double-count with `skill_view` `source=back_navigation` (see Alternatives).
  d. **PII fix: `profile_create` loses `profile_name`.** The event previously sent the child's name — the one live `/app` violation of `AGENTS.md` §4 ("No child PII in telemetry"), sitting outside the `/api` `PiiRedactionFilter` (`M2-P2-INV-001` explicitly excludes `/app`). The event is kept with **no** custom params (no substitute identity param, no birthdate).
  e. **`skill_id` joins every skill-reporting event.** `skill_view`, `graph_navigate` (from/to ids, cheaply available at the handler), and `activity_skill_open` now carry stable catalog ids alongside names, so GA4 rows join on ids.
  f. **`activities_open` counts.** The bare event moves after `getRelevantUncompletedSkills()` (throw-safe: it returns `[]` without a profile — all-zero counts are themselves signal) and gains `relevant_count` / `tier1_count` / `tier2_count` (tier-1 predicate `windowsBySkillId[s.id]`, reproduced from the `BW-DL-001` tiering; the local `hasWindowed` now derives from `tier1_count > 0`). Counts only — no skill names/lists.
  g. **`zpd_empty_state_shown`.** Fires in `applyFilter` only on the hidden→shown transition of `#zpdEmptyState` (the DOM is the guard — the element lives outside `tbody` and survives rebuilds), so repeated `applyFilter` runs while shown emit nothing. Impression semantics: a load-restore into the empty state emits one event per page load. Known undercount: switching between two profiles that both show the empty state emits only the first (no flip) — accepted.
  h. **Onboarding events.** `onboarding_shown` in `openOnboardingModal`; `onboarding_dismissed` in `closeOnboardingModal` (the single funnel for button / ✕ / backdrop) with `action: dont_show_again | closed` from the existing dismiss checkbox.
  Owner console-side work (done before this packet, recorded): event-data retention 2→14 months; 10 event-scoped custom dimensions registered (`skill_name`, `skill_category`, `skill_period`, `skill_id`, `action`, `relation`, `from_skill`, `to_skill`, `category_name`, `filter_state`) — code-side param names stay consistent with these; key events marked (`skill_complete`, `profile_create`, `filter_zpd_toggle`, `activities_open`). Registration of the NEW params (`source`, `nav_depth`, `from_skill_id`, `to_skill_id`, `relevant_count`, `tier1_count`, `tier2_count`) as custom dimensions/metrics is an **owner-side follow-up after merge**.
  **No `CACHE_VERSION` bump (owner-decided).** `index.html` is served network-first on navigations and is not in the `sw.js` precache (`OFFLINE_URLS`), so an `index.html`-only change reaches users on their next navigation without a bump. Divergence from the `GWN-DL-001` / `SLA-DL-001` precedent (which bumped to push the `#updateBanner` prompt to already-open installed PWAs) is accepted: analytics params are not a user-facing change worth an update prompt; the events simply start flowing as sessions naturally turn over.
- **Rationale.** The console-side depth (14-month retention, registered dimensions) is only as good as the payloads: without `source`, all `skill_view` rows are indistinguishable; without `nav_depth` and a chain-end event, graph-exploration chains have visible hops but invisible starts-relative-depth and ends; without stable ids, GA4 rows join on mutable display names. Threading `source` through the single `openSkillModal` choke point instruments all four open paths at one emitter (the `GWN-DL-001` single-emitter pattern). Removing `profile_name` closes the only live §4 telemetry violation at its emitter — cheaper and stronger than any redaction layer. Counts-only `activities_open` params and enum-valued `action`/`source` keep every new value low-cardinality and family-data-free.
- **No dedicated ADR by design.** ga4-instrumentation-depth has **no** vault ADR; it is measurement over existing shipped surfaces, introducing no product behavior, no architectural contract, and no new delivery mechanism. The privacy hard-constraint it enforces is `AGENTS.md` §4 (the vault delivery-contract §4.4 restatement).
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **A back/close event with `action: back | close` (or two separate events).** Rejected — every back-hop already emits `skill_view` `source=back_navigation` (item a), so a dedicated back event double-counts back navigation; the only invisible moment was the terminal close, which `skill_modal_close` captures with the abandoned depth.
  2. **Per-call-site `skill_view` emitters instead of threading `source` through `openSkillModal`.** Rejected — four emitters drift; the single choke point guarantees every open path is classified (`GWN-DL-001` precedent).
  3. **Keeping `profile_name` but hashing/truncating it.** Rejected — a derived identifier of a child's name is still family-identifying data in a third-party service; §4 says names never cross the boundary. No params at all.
  4. **Sending tier skill lists/names in `activities_open`.** Rejected — high-cardinality and unnecessary; counts answer the analytics question (how big is the relevant set and its tier split).
  5. **`CACHE_VERSION` bump per GWN/SLA precedent.** Rejected this packet (owner call) — mechanically unnecessary for a network-first, non-precached `index.html`, and an analytics-only change does not warrant prompting installed users to update.
  6. **Treating the GA events as a `theygrow_api.signals` / `parameters` surface.** Rejected — `/app` frontend product analytics via the existing `trackEvent`→gtag path, not the `/api` operability surface (`SLA-DL-001` precedent; see Effects).
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `GA-DL` namespace. Builds on `GWN-DL-001` (`graph_navigate`, the delegated chip handler + `data-relation` attributes this packet extends) and `SLA-DL-001` (`activity_skill_open`, `activateActivitySkill`), and the pre-tracked `trackEvent` surface (`skill_view` / `profile_create` / `filter_zpd_toggle` / `activities_open`, live in code before any DL record).
- **Effects.**
  - This packet's `app/` change: `app/index.html` only — `openSkillModal` signature + 4 call sites, enriched `skill_view` / `graph_navigate` / `activity_skill_open` / `activities_open` payloads, `closeSkillModal(action)` + `skill_modal_close`, `zpd_empty_state_shown` transition guard in `applyFilter`, onboarding events, `profile_create` param removal. **No `app/sw.js` change** (no `CACHE_VERSION` bump — see Decision); `app/kb-v1.json` / dataset byte-for-byte untouched.
  - **Behaviourally verified this packet** (headless gstack smoke, app served over http, `?dbg=1` debug mode, events read from the local `dataLayer` stub): all four `skill_view` sources + `back_navigation`; `graph_navigate` with `nav_depth=1` and from/to ids for `prerequisite` / `readiness` relations; `skill_modal_close` `close_button`/`nav_depth=0` and `backdrop`/`nav_depth=2`; `profile_create` payload contains **no** name (created with a real name in the form; only `ui_locale`/`app_version` enrichment present); `activities_open` counts (no-profile all-zero case and 0/10/10 tier split); `activity_skill_open` with `skill_id`; `zpd_empty_state_shown` fires once on flip, zero on three repeated `applyFilter` runs while shown, exactly once on load-restore into the empty state; `onboarding_shown` + both `onboarding_dismissed` actions; zero console errors. Static greps: `profile_name` absent from the file; each of the 9 instrumented events has exactly one emitter. Seam-honesty caveat (M4-CLOSE discipline): this proves event mechanics locally, not GA4-side data quality.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `GA4 instrumentation depth — Done` sibling block added, **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - **Operability: none** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). All events are `/app` product analytics via the existing `trackEvent` path — **not** `theygrow_api.parameters` config knobs and **not** `theygrow_api.signals` SignalSink taxonomy signals (`SLA-DL-001` precedent).
  - **No `docs/RUNBOOK.md` entry** — "what runs today" is unchanged (no new route/asset/infra; no cache bump).
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — front-only analytics milestone; nothing newly machine-enforced (`/app` unguarded; `M2-P2-INV-002` excludes it; precedent: all prior front tracks added none). The `profile_name` removal is an emitter fix, not a new machine-enforced guard.
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — the §4 no-child-PII restatement already exists there (source `AGENTS.md` §4); this packet removes the one live `/app` deviation from it but adds no new enforcement, so its status line is unchanged.
  - **No `milestonesData` / kb artifact / schema / migration change** — measurement only; no product-behavior change beyond the emitted payloads.
  - **Carried follow-ups (owner-side, after merge):** register the new params as GA4 custom dimensions/metrics; splitting `filter_zpd_toggle` into on/off events stays noted-not-done.

## A0-DL-001 — spa-polish (A0): four verified UI fixes in the monolith before the spa-split refactor

- **Date.** 2026-07-27
- **Type.** Decision record — single-packet front-only milestone; the code lands in this same packet (`app/` + this ledger reconcile), not a close-out of frozen commits. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `A0-DL` is an owner-decided dedicated namespace for the cross-cutting front-only **SPA polish** track — a thin pre-refactor milestone whose purpose is that the upcoming spa-split takes its parity/screenshot baselines from a healthy app. It is **not** track-Б, **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), **not** the icon track (`ICON-DL-001`), **not** the analytics track (`GA-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. Fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to any vault ADR. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring every prior non-spine namespace.)*
- **Decision.** Repair four owner-verified UI defects in `app/`, each at its root cause, with no restructuring of the monolith (the split is the next milestone and is explicitly out of scope):

  a. **App shell sized to the visible viewport (`100dvh`).** `body` carried `height: 100vh` + `overflow: hidden`, and the shell is `header` + `.table-wrapper` (`flex-grow:1`, the only scroller) + `footer.control-footer` (`flex-shrink:0`). Chrome/Android resolves `100vh` to the **large** viewport (URL bar retracted) while the visible viewport is the small one, so the footer laid out below the fold and `overflow:hidden` left no way to scroll the shell to it — the bottom control buttons were unreachable. iOS Safari and Comet resolve `100vh` to the visible viewport, hence the platform-specific report. Fixed by pairing the declaration — `height: 100vh; height: 100dvh;` — so supporting browsers track the dynamic viewport and non-supporting ones keep today's behaviour from the fallback line. The same pairing is applied to the mobile full-screen `.modal-content` (`height` / `max-height`), the one other declaration in the same cause class; the two `max-height: 85vh` modal caps are deliberately left alone (85% of the large viewport still fits inside the small viewport at Chrome/Android toolbar heights).

  b. **ZPD filter: the divergence was in the write, not the predicate.** `isSkillReady` is correct (a completed skill can never be ready) and `applyFilter` hid on the `data-zpd-ready` marker, yet completed skills stayed visible. Root cause: `saveCompletedSkills` was wrapped in `if (profile) { … }` and **silently no-opped when no current profile exists** (`initProfiles` never creates a default one; the header shows the `Малыш (выберите дату)` placeholder). Each tick then recomputed readiness from an empty set, so `refreshAllZpdReadiness` re-marked every previously ticked row as ready and `applyFilter` un-hid it — while its checkbox stayed `checked` in the DOM, because the row was only `display:none`, never rebuilt. Ticking N ready skills left exactly **N−1** checked-and-visible rows; the owner's report of 9 is the N=10 case, reproduced exactly (see Effects). Fixed on two edges: (1) **honest degradation at the write** (ADR-015 pattern) — `saveCompletedSkills` returns whether it actually persisted, and `toggleSkillCompletion` now checks for a profile **before** mutating or emitting: with none it restores the checkbox to its pre-click state, opens the existing `openCreateProfileModal()`, and returns, so DOM state can no longer diverge from persisted state and no `skill_complete` event is emitted for a tick that never happened; (2) **the filter derives from state, not from a stale marker** — `applyFilter` begins with `refreshAllZpdReadiness(getCompletedSkills())`, idempotent for the two callers that already refreshed (`buildTableBody`, `toggleSkillCompletion`) and load-bearing for `toggleZpdFilter`.

  c. **Install-surface icons rebuilt from an owner-supplied master; splash background white.** The reported "colored logo on a black square, no app name" splash had two independent causes. First, **all four manifest icons were RGBA with a fully transparent background** (measured: alpha extrema `(0,255)`, corner pixel `(0,0,0,0)`) — including the maskables, which the maskable contract requires to be opaque edge-to-edge; Android composites that transparent plate as black. Second, the missing app name is **not** a manifest setting: on Android 12+ the installed PWA uses the system `SplashScreen` API, which renders icon-on-`background_color` with **no** app label. The owner resolved the second by supplying `app/icons/splash-logo-name-1024.png` — a 1024×1024 vertical lockup (emblem + `TheyGrow` wordmark), opaque RGB on pure `#FFFFFF`, content within a 318 px radius of centre (verified independently this packet; inside both the Android 12+ circular splash crop and the maskable 80% safe zone at 409 px) — baking the name into the icon itself. All five install-surface icons are therefore **derived from that master** by Lanczos downscale with no geometry change (`icon-logo-192-v2`, `icon-logo-512-v2`, `maskable-192-v2`, `maskable-512-v2`, `apple-touch-icon-v2`), landing under **new filenames** per versioning-by-filename (vault ADR-024) with the superseded files left on disk and merely dereferenced (the `ICON-DL-001` precedent for `icon-192/512.png`). `background_color` `#FCFCF9` → `#FFFFFF`. The master itself is committed as the provenance source but is **not** added to `OFFLINE_URLS` — it is a build-time source, not a runtime asset. `apple-touch-icon` is included in the derivation because iOS composites a transparent apple-touch-icon onto black — the identical defect on the identical asset set. **Accepted consequence (owner decision 2026-07-26): the launcher/install icon appearance changes to the logo+name lockup.**

  d. **`theme-color` → black.** Declared in three places, all carrying the teal `#21808D` (`app/index.html:7`, `app/manifest.json`, `app/offline.html:6`); all three set to `#000000` (owner decision 2026-07-26). No `prefers-color-scheme` variant added — none exists today. The remaining `#21808D` literals inside `app/offline.html`'s own button/text styling are page styling, not `theme-color`, and are out of this packet's declared scope.

  e. **Delivery.** `app/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` `v7`→`v8` with the five icon paths swapped in `OFFLINE_URLS`. Unlike `GA-DL-001` (which skipped the bump for a network-first, non-precached `index.html`), the bump is **mandatory** here: `manifest.json` and the icons are precached cache-first, so without it installed clients would keep the teal manifest and the transparent icons indefinitely. `activate()` purges `theygrow-v7`. No `app/Dockerfile`, `app/nginx.conf`, or `app/cloudbuild.yaml` change — `COPY icons/` takes the whole directory and the generic immutable `png` location already covers the new filenames.
- **Rationale.** Fixing these in the monolith **before** the split is the whole point of the milestone: a parity baseline captured against a broken footer, a black splash, and a leaky filter would freeze those defects into the refactor's definition of "correct". Each fix is placed at its root cause rather than at the symptom — dynamic viewport units instead of a `visualViewport` listener, a write that reports failure instead of a filter that special-cases completed rows — so the behaviour survives being moved into new files by the split. The ZPD fix in particular converts a silent data-loss path into an honest one: the previous code accepted a tick, discarded it, and left the UI asserting it had been saved.
- **No dedicated ADR by design.** spa-polish has **no** vault ADR; it is defect repair over shipped surfaces, introducing no product behaviour, no architectural contract, and no new delivery mechanism. It cross-references vault **ADR-024** (versioning-by-filename, via `ICON-DL-001`) for the asset scheme, **ADR-015** (honest degradation) for the refused-tick path, **ADR-018** for the readiness predicate it leaves untouched, and `PWA-DL-001` for the `CACHE_VERSION` delivery path it rides.
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **`100svh`, or a JS `--vh` / `visualViewport` listener.** Rejected — the shell is `overflow:hidden` with an inner scroller, so the Chrome/Android toolbar never retracts and `dvh` ≈ `svh` in practice, while `dvh` self-corrects if it ever does; a resize listener would add runtime state to a file about to be split.
  2. **`viewport-fit=cover` + `env(safe-area-inset-*)`.** Rejected — insets are `0` without cover-mode, and cover-mode is precisely what would push content under the iOS home indicator, regressing the one platform that was already healthy.
  3. **Auto-create a default profile on the first tick.** Rejected — it invents a family record the owner did not ask for, to paper over a write path that should simply report failure.
  4. **Silently revert the checkbox with no affordance.** Rejected — indistinguishable from a broken checkbox; the create-profile modal already exists and names the actual precondition.
  5. **Special-case completed rows inside `applyFilter`.** Rejected — duplicates the ADR-018 predicate at a second site; recomputing readiness from the authoritative set keeps one definition of "ready".
  6. **Flatten the existing brand-logo icons onto white (the pre-correction plan).** **Superseded** by the owner-supplied lockup master — flattening would have fixed the black plate but left the splash nameless on Android 12+, where no manifest field can render the name.
  7. **Re-pad the maskable icons into the 80% safe zone as a separate geometry step.** Not needed — the master is already authored inside the safe radius, so the derivation is a pure downscale.
  8. **Overwrite the published icon filenames in place.** Rejected — ADR-024; a published immutable asset is never rewritten, it is replaced by a new filename.
  9. **Retire the superseded icon files in this packet.** Deferred — clients holding a stale manifest may still request them; dereferencing is enough, deletion is a later cleanup.
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `A0-DL` namespace. Builds on `ICON-DL-001` (the icon set and new-filename scheme this packet re-derives), `PWA-DL-001` (the `CACHE_VERSION` + `#updateBanner` delivery path the `v8` bump rides), `BW-DL-001` / `GWN-DL-001` (the ADR-018 readiness predicate, left untouched), and `GA-DL-001` (the `trackEvent` surface, from which one phantom emission is removed).
- **Effects.**
  - This packet's `app/` change: `app/index.html` (2 CSS declarations, 2 `<head>` lines, 3 JS edits), `app/manifest.json` (`theme_color`, `background_color`, 4 icon `src`), `app/offline.html` (1 `<head>` line), `app/sw.js` (`CACHE_VERSION` + 5 precache paths), and 6 added PNGs under `app/icons/` (the owner-supplied master + 5 derived `-v2` icons). `app/kb-v1.json` byte-untouched; no localStorage key, value shape, or migration touched — existing families' `childDevTracker_profiles` records are read and written exactly as before.
  - **Asset provenance (recorded).** `app/icons/splash-logo-name-1024.png` is owner-supplied, transformed from the brand logo into a logo+name lockup, opaque RGB `#FFFFFF`, verified this packet at max content radius 318 px from centre (< 409 px safe radius). The five install-surface icons are derived from it by `Image.resize((N,N), Image.LANCZOS)` + `save(optimize=True)` — reproducible from the committed master, no geometry change.
  - **Behaviourally verified this packet** (headless gstack smoke, `app/` served over http at a 412×760 mobile viewport, zero console errors throughout): **(a)** `document.body.clientHeight === innerHeight === 760` and `footer.control-footer` bottom `= 760` (fully on-screen, top at 691); `CSS.supports('height','100dvh')` true and the CSSOM shows `height: 100dvh` winning in both the `body` and `.modal-content` rules. **(b) Root cause reproduced on the pristine pre-fix tree** (`ba64a1d:app/index.html` served on a separate origin): with no profile, ticking 10 ZPD-ready skills and enabling the filter left exactly **9** checked-and-visible rows — the reported number, reproduced. Post-fix, the identical scenario leaves **0** checked in the DOM (each tick refused and reverted, `localStorage` untouched, create-profile modal opened, and no `skill_complete` in the `?dbg=1` dataLayer). With a profile: 10 ticks persist to `completedSkills`, the active filter shows 13 rows of which **0** are checked, every visible row is `data-zpd-ready="true"`, and `0` carry `skill-completed`; across a reload (filter state restored from localStorage) and four off/on toggles the visible-checked count stays `0` (`[off,174,10] → [on,13,0]` twice); unticking a completed skill returns it to view only because it is genuinely ready, still with `0` visible-checked. **(c)** Each derived icon verified `RGB`, correct size, corner pixel `#FFFFFF`; all four manifest `src` and all 11 `OFFLINE_URLS` paths fetch `200`; the master is absent from `OFFLINE_URLS`. **(d)** All three `theme-color` sites read `#000000` and no `#21808D` remains outside `offline.html`'s page styling. Repo-side: `node --check` clean on the extracted inline scripts, `pre-commit run --all-files` green, working tree limited to the declared change set (gstack `.gstack/` + `.gitignore` residue reverted per harness discipline).
  - **Seam-honesty caveat** (M4-CLOSE discipline): the headless smoke proves DOM/state mechanics and layout arithmetic. It **cannot** prove fix (a) on the real defect surface — a headless browser has no retracting Chrome/Android toolbar — nor can it show an Android splash. Both remain **owner on-device smoke steps**, named below.
  - **Operability: none** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). The `CACHE_VERSION` bump is mechanical operation of the existing front-side knob (`changed_in: PWA-DL-001`), not a new one; theme/background colours and icon filenames are static manifest/asset literals on the front-only surface, whose typed-config home (`theygrow_api.parameters`) is in `/api`, out of this perimeter (`VDK-DL-001` / `SLA-DL-001` precedent). No new GA4 event and no `theygrow_api.signals` taxonomy signal; the only telemetry-shaped change is the **removal** of a `skill_complete` emission for a tick that was never persisted.
  - `docs/execution-map.md` reconciled this packet: a `SPA polish (A0) — Done` sibling block added, **outside** the M1–M5 engine spine.
  - `docs/RUNBOOK.md` reconciled this packet: the app-icons line rewritten for the `-v2` set and the lockup master; the `CACHE_VERSION v7` reference advanced to `v8`.
  - **No `docs/INVARIANTS.md` entry** — front-only defect repair; nothing newly machine-enforced (`/app` has no CI gate; every prior front track added none).
  - **No `docs/RUNTIME-INVARIANTS.md` entry** — no §2/§4 product-invariant status changes; the honest-degradation edge added at the tick path is an application of `AGENTS.md` §2, not a new enforcement.
  - **Owner smoke before promotion (on-device, per the ADR-020 gate):** on Chrome/Android, both footer control buttons reachable with the address bar shown; no regression on iOS Safari and Comet; status/address bar legible in black against the white app header; and — after **uninstalling and reinstalling** the PWA — the splash showing the logo+name lockup on white. The reinstall matters: an installed WebAPK only picks up manifest changes on Chrome's own update check, which can lag ~a day, so an un-reinstalled device is not evidence of failure.
  - **Carried follow-ups:** retire the now-dereferenced icon files (`icon-logo-192/512`, `maskable-192/512`, `apple-touch-icon`, plus the older `icon-192/512`, `icon-master-1024`) in a later cleanup; the accordion-vs-`.hidden` bookkeeping divergence found while tracing this packet (a collapsed mobile category hides its rows with inline `display:none`, which `applyFilter`'s `visibleSkillCount` and `hideEmptyCategories` do not count, so the honest ZPD empty state can be suppressed) — **logged, not fixed**; the wider `vh`→`dvh` sweep (the two `85vh` modal caps, `calc(100vw - 76px)` column math) and the purple-UI vs teal-`offline.html` palette split stay noted-not-done.
