## A1-DL-001 — spa-split (A1) P0: the ZPD filter suspends the mobile accordion, and the empty state stays honest

- **Date.** 2026-07-27
- **Type.** Decision record — design fork accepted at P0 review; the code landed at `3e11a1a` ahead of this entry. *(Namespace note: this log's "Entry format" header describes ids as `M{N}-DL-{NNN}`; `A1-DL` is the dedicated namespace for the **spa-split** milestone — the buildless native ES-module split of `app/index.html`. It is **not** the `A0-DL` polish track, **not** the front delivery track (`PWA-DL-001`), and **not** the M1–M5 engine spine. Fresh per-namespace sequence (`001`), deliberately **not** number-pinned to any vault ADR. The format header is intentionally left unedited this packet — mirroring every prior non-spine namespace.)*
- **Provenance.** `A0-DL-001` carried this defect forward explicitly as a **logged, not fixed** follow-up: "a collapsed mobile category hides its rows with inline `display:none`, which `applyFilter`'s `visibleSkillCount` and `hideEmptyCategories` do not count, so the honest ZPD empty state can be suppressed." A1-P0 is the sanctioned exception to A1's zero-product-change rule: it lands **before** the parity suite so the suite baselines a healthy app, exactly as A0 was sequenced before A1.
- **Decision.** Separate the accordion's **presentation layer** from the family's **stored intent**, and let an active ZPD filter suspend the former without mutating the latter:

  a. **`applyAccordionVisibility(options)` becomes the single owner of collapsed-ness.** With the ZPD filter active every category renders expanded; with it inactive, each category returns to `isCategoryCollapsedByIntent(name, index)` — the saved value, or the default (first category open, rest closed). Stored intent is never overwritten by the filter, so switching the filter off restores exactly what the family chose.

  b. **Writes are per-row and explicit.** `saveCategoryStates()` (a DOM sweep that inferred intent from the `.collapsed` class) is replaced by `saveCategoryState(categoryRow, isCollapsed)`. The sweep became actively destructive once presentation could legitimately diverge from intent: one manual toggle under an active filter would have written "expanded" for *every* category, erasing the family's settings.

  c. **One key function.** `categoryKey(categoryRow)` is the single source for the `categoryCollapsedStates` key, so the write key and the read key cannot drift. The `[done/total]` counter is drawn by CSS (`content: attr(data-skills-count)`) and never reaches `textContent`; the strip is retained for compatibility with previously saved keys.

  d. **`accordionSyncedForFilter` guards re-entry.** Without `{force: true}` the sync is skipped when the filter state has not changed, so an ordinary `applyFilter()` — a skill tick, say — cannot silently undo a manual collapse the user performed while the filter was on.

  e. **The honest empty state keeps counting `.hidden`, not collapsed-ness.** `applyAccordionVisibility()` is sequenced **last**, after the visibility bookkeeping above it. Counting collapsed rows as absent would make "нет готовых навыков" a lie while ready skills sat one chevron-tap away (ADR-018).
- **Rationale.** The filter is a just-expressed intent to see a short list; hiding its results behind collapsed headers contradicts the request that produced them — and that contradiction is precisely what rendered a visually empty table with no honest empty state. Fixing it at the layer boundary (presentation vs intent) rather than at the symptom means the behaviour survives being moved into new files by the split, which is the whole point of doing it before the extraction rather than after.
- **No dedicated ADR by design.** Defect repair over a shipped surface, introducing no product behaviour, no architectural contract and no new delivery mechanism. Cross-references vault **ADR-018** (the readiness predicate, untouched) and `A0-DL-001` (the provenance above).
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Count collapsed rows as hidden in `visibleSkillCount`.** Rejected — it makes the empty state claim there are no ready skills when there are; the message would be false exactly when it matters.
  2. **Force-expand categories and overwrite the saved state.** Rejected — it destroys a family setting to fix a rendering concern, and the setting cannot be recovered when the filter goes off.
  3. **Disable the accordion entirely on mobile while filtering.** Rejected — the chevrons would remain visible but inert, which reads as broken.
  4. **Keep the `saveCategoryStates()` DOM sweep and special-case the filter inside it.** Rejected — it keeps intent derived from presentation, which is the defect; the sweep is the thing that had to go.
- **Supersedes.** None — first entry in the `A1-DL` namespace. Builds on `A0-DL-001` (which logged this defect and deferred it) and vault `ADR-018` (the readiness predicate it leaves untouched).
- **Effects.**
  - `app/index.html` only, `+78/−31` @ `3e11a1a`. No `app/sw.js`, `app/manifest.json`, `app/nginx.conf`, `app/Dockerfile` or `app/cloudbuild.yaml` change; `app/kb-v1.json` byte-untouched; no localStorage key or value shape touched (`milestones_accordion_states` keeps its existing shape and keys).
  - **No `CACHE_VERSION` bump** — `index.html` is network-first and not precached by name (`OFFLINE_URLS` carries `/`, not `/index.html`), the `GA-DL-001` precedent.
  - **Operability: none** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). No new knob, no new GA4 event, no `theygrow_api.signals` taxonomy signal.
  - **Verification status.** Owner-verified on-device at P0 review. This entry is the ledger record of a fork accepted then; A1-P1 lands the automated gate that will hold it from here on — `A1-P1-INV-001` baselines this fixed state, and the mobile accordion path is covered at the 412×760 viewport.

---

## A1-DL-002 — spa-split (A1) P1: a three-level parity suite becomes the acceptance gate, and `/app` gets its first CI gate

- **Date.** 2026-07-27
- **Type.** Decision record — the packet's code and this entry land together.
- **Decision.** Land the full three-level parity threshold (vault ADR-037 §3) as a Playwright suite owned by the `app/` subtree, wired as a second CI job, **before** any module extraction. Nothing in `app/` that ships is modified by this packet.

  a. **Level (a) — DOM snapshot.** `header`, `#mainTable` (`#tableHead` + `#tableBody`), `footer.control-footer`, `#zpdEmptyState`, and the four modals (`#createProfileModal`, `#skillModal`, `#activitiesModal`, `#onboardingModal`), captured across four boot states (seeded profile / no profile / ZPD filter active / first run) at two viewports. Three artifact kinds chosen by size so failures stay readable: normalized HTML for small containers, a structural JSON digest for `#tableBody` (per row: skill id, `data-zpd-ready`, month-window class runs, checkbox state — 174 rows stay diffable), and SHA-256 for the long tail, including **all 174 skill-modal bodies**. This is the direct automation of the method `VDK-P3` ran by hand.

  b. **Level (b) — visual regression.** `toHaveScreenshot` over the same containers plus full-shell shots at 1280×800 and 412×760, with animations disabled and a zero-pixel tolerance. The platform is fixed by the pinned Playwright container, not by the CI runner image.

  c. **Level (c) — behavioral smoke.** Main flow (tick → ZPD recompute across dependents → persist → survive reload), the A1-P0 honest-degradation refusal, reload from seeded storage, the activity→skill deep link and its modal stack, service-worker registration + offline boot from precache + the full PWA update flow, and the kb-load error path.

  d. **Baselines are never written implicitly.** `updateSnapshots: 'none'` means a missing or changed baseline **fails**; the only way to write one is `scripts/parity-suite.sh --update-snapshots`.
- **Rationale.** A1 is a refactor whose entire claim is "nothing changed". That claim is worth exactly as much as the machinery able to falsify it, so the gate lands before the first extraction rather than alongside it. Two properties of the app forced most of the design: `calculateAge`/`calculateAgeInMonths` read `new Date()`, so every rendered surface drifts daily unless the clock is pinned; and `setFixedSkillColumnWidth` measures text, so layout is font-metric dependent and screenshots are only reproducible on a fixed platform.
- **No dedicated ADR by design at the repo layer.** The suite realizes vault **ADR-037 §3** (the three-level threshold) and is the first consumer of **ADR-030 §2.1** (permanent CI parity/smoke suite).
- **Alternatives considered.**
  1. **Serve the app in CI from the real `app/Dockerfile` nginx image.** Rejected — the PWA update flow needs a byte-different `/sw.js` on the **same origin** while `app/sw.js` and `CACHE_VERSION` stay untouched; an immutable container cannot provide that, and a second origin registers a different worker, making the test meaningless. `app/tests/server.js` mirrors `app/nginx.conf` instead, and `tests/delivery-contract.spec.js` parses the real `nginx.conf` and fails on divergence — the drift risk becomes a red test rather than silent rot.
  2. **Pin `ubuntu-24.04` and `npx playwright install --with-deps`.** Rejected for screenshots — the runner image's font set changes underneath the baselines without notice. The container pins Chromium and the fonts together.
  3. **Hashes only for every DOM artifact (the literal `VDK-P3` method).** Rejected where size permits — `VDK-P3` compared two live origins, so a hash was all it could use; here the comparand is committed, and a failing hash names nothing.
  4. **A top-level `tests/` directory with a repo-root `package.json`.** Rejected — `AGENTS.md` §8 has no top-level `tests/`, and M2-P3 established that build/test config lives in the owning subtree (`api/tests/`, `api/Dockerfile`).
  5. **Retries to absorb flake.** Rejected — a retry lets a flaky parity assertion pass on the second attempt, hiding exactly the instability the gate exists to surface. `retries: 0`.
  6. **A `paths: [app/**]` filter, or `needs: quality`, on the CI job.** Both are the documented mitigations if job time becomes a problem; neither is applied now, so the gate is uniform and a Python lint failure cannot mask a frontend regression.
- **Supersedes.** None. Builds on `A1-DL-001` and `A0-DL-001` (the fixed state it baselines), `VDK-DL-001` (the per-section parity method it automates), and `PWA-DL-001` (the `CACHE_VERSION` + `#updateBanner` delivery path the update-flow test exercises without modifying).
- **Effects.**
  - **Added (dev/CI only):** `app/package.json` + `app/package-lock.json` (one exact-pinned dependency, `@playwright/test` 1.61.1), `app/playwright.config.js`, `app/tests/**` (server, support helpers, four specs, committed baselines), `app/.dockerignore`, `scripts/parity-suite.sh`.
  - **Changed:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (new `parity` job), `.pre-commit-config.yaml` (baseline directory added to the two rewriting-hook excludes), `.gitignore` (run output), and this ledger + `docs/INVARIANTS.md` + `docs/execution-map.md` + `docs/RUNBOOK.md`.
  - **Untouched, verified byte-identical:** `app/index.html`, `app/sw.js`, `app/kb-v1.json`, `app/manifest.json`, `app/offline.html`, `app/nginx.conf`, `app/Dockerfile`, `app/cloudbuild.yaml`. `CACHE_VERSION` stays `v8` and no precached file changes, so no installed client is affected. The production delivery channel remains buildless: the image is built from an explicit `COPY` list that excludes every file added here, and `app/.dockerignore` keeps them out of the build context as well (the image is byte-identical with or without it).
  - **First non-Python CI gate in this repo.** `A0-DL-001` recorded that "`/app` has no CI gate; every prior front track added none" — that is no longer true from this packet.
  - **Operability: none** (ADR-013 / contract §4.7). Test infrastructure with no product surface: no `theygrow_api.parameters` knob, no `theygrow_api.signals` taxonomy signal, no new GA4 event, and **no change to production emission**. The suite only *observes* `window.dataLayer` through the existing inline `gtag` shim. Two §4-relevant notes: the suite intercepts and stubs the analytics beacon, so CI performs no third-party egress and emits no telemetry; and the sw-bump control is a cookie-keyed branch inside `app/tests/server.js` alone — absent from `app/nginx.conf`, absent from `app/Dockerfile`, and unreachable in production.
  - **Seam-honesty caveat** (M4-CLOSE discipline): the suite proves DOM/state mechanics, delivery-header mechanics and layout arithmetic in one pinned headless browser. It does **not** prove real-device rendering, cross-browser behaviour (Chromium only — a platform-fixed screenshot gate requires it), or on-device PWA install/splash behaviour. Those remain owner on-device smoke steps per the ADR-020 gate.
  - **Deliberately deferred to the split packets, diagnosed here:** extracted `.js`/`.css` fall into the generic `immutable, max-age=2592000` location in `app/nginx.conf`, so split assets need versioning-by-filename (vault ADR-024) or installed clients will pin stale modules; and `app/Dockerfile` has **no wildcard `COPY`**, so each new module directory needs an explicit line or it silently will not ship.
